find_matching_commit(&commit, &other_workspace, &other_repo)?\n            {\n                let new_desc = append_ochid_trailer(\n                    desc,\n                    &other_prefix,\n                    &matched_id,\n                    args.id_len,\n                );\n                let first_line = desc.lines().next().unwrap_or(\"\");\n                let short_matched = &matched_id[..matched_id.len().min(args.id_len)];\n                if !args.no_dry_run {\n                    println!(\"add  {change_short}  {first_line}  [missing]\");\n                    println!(\"     -> ochid: {other_prefix}{short_matched}\");\n                } else {\n                    let status = Command::new(\"jj\")\n                        .arg(\"describe\")\n                        .arg(\"-m\")\n                        .arg(&new_desc)\n                        .arg(\"-r\")\n                        .arg(commit_id.hex())\n                        .arg(\"-R\")\n                        .arg(&args.repo)\n                        .arg(\"--ignore-immutable\")\n                        .stdout(std::process::Stdio::null())\n                        .stderr(std::process::Stdio::inherit())\n                        .status()?;\n                    if !status.success() {\n                        return Err(format!(\n                            \"jj describe failed for {} (exit {})\",\n                            change_short,\n                            status.code().unwrap_or(-1)\n                        )\n                        .into());\n                    }\n                    println!(\"added {change_short}  {first_line}\");\n                }\n                fixed += 1;\n                continue;\n            } else {\n                skipped += 1;\n                if !args.no_dry_run {\n                    println!(\"skip {change_short}  (no ochid, no match in other repo)\");\n                }\n                continue;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // No ochid trailer — nothing to fix\n            skipped += 1;\n            if !args.no_dry_run {\n                println!(\"skip {change_short}  (no ochid trailer)\");\n            }\n            continue;\n        };"
}

> AGENT

Looking back at the conversation, I ran this command earlier:

```
cd /home/wink/data/prgs/rust/vc-x1/.claude && vc-x1 fix-ochid -r @.. --other-repo ../
```

I used `cd` to change into `.claude` for the smoke test, and the working directory persisted after that. I should have used an absolute path or run the command without `cd`, like:

```
vc-x1 fix-ochid -r @.. --other-repo ../ -R /home/wink/data/prgs/rust/vc-x1/.claude
```

Or just `cd` back afterward. The tool docs even say "Try to maintain your current working directory throughout the session by using absolute paths and avoiding usage of `cd`."

The working\ndirectory persists between Bash calls, and losing track of cwd causes\ndownstream commands to fail with confusing errors.\n\n**Why:** cd'd into `.claude` for a smoke test, then subsequent `vc-x1 chid -R .,.claude`\nfailed because `.claude/.claude` doesn't exist. Wasted time diagnosing.\n\n**How to apply:** Use absolute paths or `-R` flags to target other directories.\nIf a command must run from a specific directory, use a subshell:\n`(cd /path && command)` so cwd is not affected.\n"

```
     1→# Memory
     2→
     3→## User Preferences
     4→
     5→- **Commit approval**: Always present the full commit commands to the user for physical review BEFORE executing them. Do not run jj commit / git commit until the user explicitly approves.
     6→- **Versioning**: Every plan must start with a version bump per notes/README.md "Versioning during development". Get user approval on single-step vs multi-step approach before starting.
     7→- **Pre-commit checklist**: Before committing, run: tests, clippy, fmt, install the app, then retest.
     8→
     9→## Feedback
    10→
    11→- [Always include ochid trailer in commits](feedback_ochid_trailer.md)
    12→- [Always commit .claude repo before finalize](feedback_claude_commit_before_finalize.md)
    13→- [Skip .claude push before finalize — finalize handles it](feedback_no_claude_push.md)
    14→- [Finalize must be absolute last action — only status line after](feedback_finalize_last.md)
    15→- [Use --ignore-immutable for late squash after push](feedback_squash_ignore_immutable.md)
    16→- [Use vc-x1 chid to retrieve changeIDs, not jj log](feedback_use_chid.md)
    17→- [Recognize when stuck — 5+ min thinking on simple task means stuck](feedback_stuck_detection.md)
    18→- [No --allow-new flag for jj git push](feedback_jj_no_allow_new.md)
    19→- [No text output after finalize — trailing writes miss squash window](feedback_no_finalize_echo.md)
    20→- [Ask for clarification on ambiguous input instead of spinning](feedback_ask_on_ambiguity.md)
    21→- ["finalize steps" triggers full session-end workflow](feedback_finalize_steps.md)
    22→- [Use relative paths in commands for brevity](feedback_relative_paths.md)
    23→- [Use -L for ochid retrieval — clean parseable output](feedback_use_no_label.md)
    24→
    25→## Lessons Learned
    26→
    27→- **Avoid duplicate commits in .claude repo**: Committing the bot session repo multiple times in one session can create duplicate-titled commits with other session commits interleaved, making squashing non-trivial. Prefer a single commit to .claude at session end.
    28→- **ochid trailers need updating after squash**: When squashing changes the target commit in .claude, the ochid reference in the app repo must be updated to point at the correct changeID.
    29→- **Don't modify .claude repo programmatically mid-session**: Session data updates continuously, so bot-initiated changes to .claude can create circular modifications.
    30→

```
     1→# vc-x1
     2→
     3→- [Overview](#vc-x1)
     4→- [Usage](#usage)
     5→  - [Revision shortcuts](#revision-shortcuts)
     6→  - [finalize](#finalize)
     7→  - [Testing finalize](#testing-finalize)
     8→- [Cross-repo Linking with Git Trailers](#cross-repo-linking-with-git-trailers)
     9→- [jj Tips for Git Users](#jj-tips-for-git-users)
    10→- [License](#license)
    11→
    12→This is experiment 1 to explore creating a Vibe Coding (vc) environment.
    13→We will investigate ways of using the dual jj-git repo concept, explored
    14→in [hw-jjg-bot](https://github.com/winksaville/hw-jjg-bot.git) to
    15→initially make it easy to see how the code base evolved. This is
    16→made possible by the fact that we have two repos one with the code
    17→and one with the conversation with the bot.
    18→
    19→I've chosen the jj-git environment because jj provides the concept that
    20→each commit has an immutable changeID as well as the mutable commitID
    21→of git. The idea is that each commit made on repo A writes the
    22→changeID in the commit message to repo B. Thus there is a cross reference
    23→between the two repos and this will allow vc-x1 to show how the repo
    24→evolved and the entity (bot or human) can more clearly understand **how** and
    25→most importantly **why** the code evolved.
    26→
    27→The solution space is wide open, from trivial CLI, web or app based
    28→(mobile/non-mobile). In addition, I could see this as an extension to
    29→existing programming editors like vscode and zed or even creating our
    30→own IDE for vc.
    31→
    32→See [Initial commit with dual jj-git repos](./notes/chores-01.md#initial-commit-with-dual-jj-git-repos)
    33→for how the initial commit was created with the dual jj-git repos. After
    34→doing so and I then created this README.md file.
    35→
    36→## Usage
    37→
    38→```
    39→vc-x1 list [-r REVISION] [-n COMMITS]  # List commits in a jj repo
    40→vc-x1 desc [-r REVISION] [-n COMMITS]  # Show full description of a commit
    41→vc-x1 chid [-r REVISION] [-n COMMITS]  # Print changeID(s) for a revision
    42→vc-x1 show [-r REVISION] [-n COMMITS]  # Show commit details and diff summary
    43→vc-x1 finalize --bookmark <B> [OPTS]   # Squash working copy into target
    44→vc-x1 --version                        # Print version
    45→vc-x1 --help                           # Print help
    46→```
    47→
    48→**The `..` notation:** dots on the revision show which direction to
    49→list commits:
    50→
    51→- `x..` — x at top, ancestors below (older commits)
    52→- `..x` — descendants above (newer commits), x at bottom
    53→- `..x..` — both directions, x in the middle
    54→
    55→COMMITS is the total number of commits to show (x is always included).
    56→Without dots, `x` with a count defaults to `x..` (ancestors).
    57→For `..x..`, the budget is split: ancestors get the extra on odd counts.
    58→
    59→```
    60→vc-x1 list -r @              # just @ (1 commit)
    61→vc-x1 list -r @.. -n 5       # @ + 4 ancestors (5 commits, @ at top)
    62→vc-x1 list -r ..@ -n 3       # 2 descendants + @ (3 commits, @ at bottom)
    63→vc-x1 list -r ..@.. -n 3     # 1 descendant, @, 1 ancestor (3 commits)
    64→vc-x1 list -r ..@.. -n 4     # 1 descendant, @, 2 ancestors (4 commits)
    65→```
    66→
    67→### Positional shorthand
    68→
    69→REVISION and COMMITS can be given as positional arguments, omitting
    70→`-r` and `-n`:
    71→
    72→```
    73→vc-x1 list x                # just x (1 commit)
    74→vc-x1 list x 5              # x.. 5 → x + 4 ancestors (5 commits)
    75→vc-x1 list x.. 5            # same as above
    76→vc-x1 list ..x 3            # 2 descendants + x (3 commits)
    77→vc-x1 list ..x.. 3          # 1 descendant, x, 1 ancestor (3 commits)
    78→vc-x1 list ..x.. 1          # just x (1 commit)
    79→```
    80→
    81→**Defaults:** with no arguments, REVISION is `@` and COMMITS is 1
    82→(just the working copy).
    83→
    84→Named flags `-r`/`--revision`, `-n`/`--commits`, and `-R`/`--repo`
    85→take precedence over positional arguments.
    86→
    87→### Multi-repo queries
    88→
    89→The `-R`/`--repo` flag can be repeated or comma-separated to query
    90→multiple repos at once. When multiple repos are given, output is
    91→labeled with bold `=== path ===` headers by default:
    92→
    93→```
    94→vc-x1 chid -R . -R .claude      # repeated flag
    95→vc-x1 chid -R .,.claude         # comma-separated
    96→vc-x1 list @.. 3 -R .,.claude   # works with all read-only subcommands
    97→```
    98→
    99→Control the label between repos with `-l`/`--label` and `-L`/`--no-label`:
   100→
   101→```
   102→vc-x1 chid -R .,.claude            # default label: === path ===
   103→vc-x1 chid -R .,.claude -l "---"   # custom label:  --- path ---
   104→vc-x1 chid -R .,.claude -L         # no label (raw output)
   105→```
   106→
   107→Examples:
   108→
   109→```
   110→$ vc-x1 chid -R . -R .claude
   111→=== . ===
   112→kwoyvposvsmv
   113→
   114→=== .claude ===
   115→zzpksklxyrnw
   116→
   117→$ vc-x1 chid -R . -R .claude -L
   118→kwoyvposvsmv
   119→zzpksklxyrnw
   120→
   121→$ vc-x1 chid -R .,.claude
   122→=== . ===
   123→kwoyvposvsmv
   124→
   125→=== .claude ===
   126→zzpksklxyrnw
   127→
   128→$ vc-x1 chid -R .,.claude -L
   129→kwoyvposvsmv
   130→zzpksklxyrnw
   131→
   132→$ vc-x1 list @.. 3 -R .,.claude
   133→=== . ===
   134→kwoyvposvsmv eae175c3c1b5 (no description set)
   135→pqxtxxpnsmot b0c46930a640 Reorganize notes (0.19.1)
   136→ssokyszzwsxw eeb15bc6d839 Unify .. notation and CLI (0.19.0)
   137→
   138→=== .claude ===
   139→zzpksklxyrnw 2388185e42ee (no description set)
   140→tzupykyyvnrp 81ce22e34d41 Reorganize notes (0.19.1)
   141→slmkmroqtqtp 1c33ccaa567d Unify .. notation and CLI (0.19.0)
   142→
   143→$ vc-x1 chid -R .,.claude -L
   144→kwoyvposvsmv
   145→zzpksklxyrnw
   146→
   147→$ vc-x1 desc -r @- -R .,.claude
   148→=== . ===
   149→pqxtxxpnsmot b0c46930a640 Reorganize notes: move older done items to done.md, update todos (0.19.1)
   150→
   151→    Move completed items [1]-[13] and their references from todo.md to done.md.
   152→    Replace completed jj-organization todo with new items.
   153→
   154→    ochid: tzupykyyvnrp
   155→
   156→=== .claude ===
   157→tzupykyyvnrp 81ce22e34d41 Reorganize notes: move older done items to done.md, update todos (0.19.1)
   158→
   159→    Session: reviewed and committed notes reorganization for 0.19.1.
   160→
   161→    ochid: pqxtxxpnsmot
   162→
   163→$ vc-x1 chid -R .,.claude -l "---"
   164→--- . ---
   165→kwoyvposvsmv
   166→
   167→--- .claude ---
   168→zzpksklxyrnw
   169→```
   170→
   171→Most decoration strings work unquoted (`---`, `===`, `>>>`, `:::`,
   172→`+++`). Use single quotes for strings containing shell metacharacters
   173→like `*`, `!`, `#`, `$`, or `~` (e.g. `-l '***'`). Double quotes
   174→won't protect against `!` (bash history expansion).
   175→
   176→With a single repo (or no `-R`), no label is printed — backward
   177→compatible with previous behavior. Multi-repo is supported for
   178→`chid`, `desc`, `list`, and `show`. `finalize` remains single-repo.
   179→
   180→### finalize
   181→
   182→Solves the session repo coherence problem: when the bot commits `.claude`,
   183→the act of committing generates more session data. `finalize` waits,
   184→then squashes the trailing writes into the target commit.
   185→
   186→By default finalize runs in the foreground. Use `--detach` to run in
   187→the background (the bot uses this so the session can end immediately).
   188→
   189→The bot's last action in a session:
   190→
   191→```
   192→vc-x1 finalize --repo .claude --bookmark main --delay 10 --detach --push
   193→```
   194→
   195→If there is non-written session data after a session ends (e.g.
   196→finalize failed or was skipped), run it manually:
   197→
   198→```
   199→vc-x1 finalize --repo .claude --bookmark main --push
   200→```
   201→
   202→This runs in the foreground, squashes, advances the bookmark, and pushes.
   203→
   204→See [finalize subcommand](./notes/chores-01.md#finalize-subcommand-for-session-repo-coherence)
   205→for full option reference and design details.
   206→
   207→### Testing finalize
   208→
   209→Always test against a throwaway jj repo, never the live workspace.
   210→Use `mktemp -d` for unique directories so results can't be confused
   211→with a previous run:
   212→
   213→```bash
   214→# Create and init a temp repo
   215→dir=$(mktemp -d /tmp/vc-x1-test-XXXXXX)
   216→(cd "$dir" && jj git init)
   217→
   218→# Foreground (default, blocks, logs to test dir)
   219→vc-x1 finalize --repo "$dir" --bookmark main --log "$dir/finalize.log"
   220→cat "$dir/finalize.log"
   221→
   222→# Detached (returns immediately, child logs to test dir)
   223→dir2=$(mktemp -d /tmp/vc-x1-test-XXXXXX)
   224→(cd "$dir2" && jj git init)
   225→vc-x1 finalize --detach --repo "$dir2" --bookmark main --log "$dir2/finalize.log"
   226→cat "$dir2/finalize.log"
   227→```
   228→
   229→The log file shows timestamped (nanoseconds) entries with PIDs, covering
   230→the full flow: `main` entry/exit, `finalize` entry/exit, `detach`
   231→spawn, and `finalize_exec` in the child process.
   232→
   233→## Cross-repo Linking with Git Trailers
   234→
   235→Commits in each repo use [git trailers](https://git-scm.com/docs/git-interpret-trailers)
   236→to cross-reference their counterpart in the other repo. The `ochid`
   237→(Other Change ID) trailer contains a workspace-root-relative path
   238→and jj changeID:
   239→
   240→```
   241→ochid: /.claude/xvzvruqo   # points to a .claude repo change
   242→ochid: /wtpmottv            # points to an app repo change
   243→```
   244→
   245→Paths always start with `/` (the workspace root, i.e. vc-x1).
   246→Each repo has a `.vc-config.toml` that identifies its location
   247→within the workspace, so tools can resolve these paths locally.
   248→
   249→For full details see:
   250→- [Git trailer convention](./notes/chores-01.md#git-trailer-convention)
   251→  — [ochid (Other Change ID)](./notes/chores-01.md#ochid-other-change-id)
   252→  — [ChangeID path syntax](./notes/chores-01.md#changeid-path-syntax)
   253→  — [.vc-config.toml](./notes/chores-01.md#vc-configtoml)
   254→
   255→## jj Tips for Git Users
   256→
   257→If you're coming from git, jj's log output can be surprising compared to
   258→tools like `gitk --all`.
   259→
   260→### Why `jj log` shows fewer commits than `gitk`
   261→
   262→jj tracks *changes* (identified by change IDs), not individual git commits.
   263→When you rewrite a change (`jj describe`, `jj rebase`, `jj squash`, etc.),
   264→jj creates a new git commit and keeps the old one under `refs/jj/keep/*` as
   265→undo history. `gitk --all` sees all of these obsolete commits; `jj log` only
   266→shows the current version of each change.
   267→
   268→### Useful commands
   269→
   270→| Command | Description |
   271→|---------|-------------|
   272→| `jj log` | Show recent visible commits (default revset) |
   273→| `jj log -r ::@` | Show **all** ancestors of the working copy |
   274→| `jj log -r 'all()'` | Show all non-hidden commits (needed if you have multiple heads/branches) |
   275→| `jj obslog -r <change-id>` | Show the evolution history of a single change |
   276→| `jj op log` | Show operation history (each rewrite operation) |
   277→
   278→In a single-branch workflow, `jj log -r ::@` and `jj log -r 'all()'` give
   279→the same result. Use `all()` when you have multiple branches or heads.
   280→

```

  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/vc-x1/README.md",
  "old_string": "### finalize",
  "new_string": "### fix-ochid\n\nValidates and fixes ochid trailers across commit history. Checks three\nproperties: correct path prefix, correct changeID length, and that the\nID resolves in the other repo. Default is dry-run — use `--no-dry-run`\nto write changes.\n\n```\n# Dry-run: show what would be fixed\nvc-x1 fix-ochid -r @.. --other-repo .claude\n\n# Actually fix\nvc-x1 fix-ochid -r @.. --other-repo .claude --no-dry-run\n\n# Add missing ochid trailers by matching title + timestamp (within 60s)\nvc-x1 fix-ochid -r @.. --other-repo .claude --add-missing\n\n# Use a fallback for IDs not found in other repo\nvc-x1 fix-ochid -r @.. --other-repo .claude --fallback /.claude/lost\n```\n\nKey flags:\n\n| Flag | Description |\n|------|-------------|\n| `--other-repo <PATH>` | Path to the counterpart repo (required) |\n| `--no-dry-run` | Write fixes (default is dry-run) |\n| `--add-missing` | Infer and add ochid for commits without one |\n| `--fallback <VALUE>` | Replacement for IDs not found in other repo |\n| `--id-len <N>` | Expected changeID length (default 12) |\n| `--title <TEXT>` | Replace commit title at the same time |\n\nThe `--add-missing` flag matches commits by exact title and committer\ntimestamp within 60 seconds. It only adds the trailer when exactly one\nmatch is found — ambiguous or zero matches are skipped.\n\n### finalize"
}
```

> AGENT

I see the issues:

1. **Ordering**: 0.23.0 appears before 0.22.2 — they should be in version order
2. **Heading levels**: The versioned entries (0.20.1–0.23.0) are all `###` under topic groupings like `## Test dispersal and ochid list column`. Your suggestion is that `0.xx.0` (minor version bumps = new features) should be `##` level, with patch versions as `###` under them.

So the structure would be:
```
## 0.22.0 — Add fix-ochid subcommand
### 0.22.1 — Fix fix-ochid prefix bug
### 0.22.2 — Fix fix-ochid short ID extension
## 0.23.0 — Add --add-missing to fix-ochid
```

And the older topic-based groupings (`## Test dispersal and ochid list column`, `## Show subcommand`) would become `## 0.20.0`, `## 0.18.0`, etc.

Should I restructure the whole versioned section this way, or just fix the 0.22–0.23 area?

   404→## Show subcommand (0.18.0)
   405→
   406→### 0.18.0 — Initial show subcommand
   407→
   408→Implement `vc-x1 show` matching `jj show` output: commit/change IDs,
   409→bookmarks (local + remote), author/committer with timestamps,
   410→indented description, and file-level diff summary (Added/Modified/Removed).
   411→Uses `TreeDiffIterator` for sync tree diffing via jj-lib.
   412→
   413→### 0.18.1 — Flesh out show header
   414→
   415→Show header now has gitk-style fields: Ids, Author, Committer, Parent,
   416→Child, Branches (ancestry-based), Follows/Precedes (nearest tags),
   417→Description (body only). Supports `..` notation for multi-commit display
   418→with separators. File list: `-f` flag (default 50, `0`=none, `all`=unlimited)
   419→with first/last split when truncated. Remove `--indent` from desc,
   420→hardcode 4-space indent in both desc and show.
   421→
   422→### 0.19.0 — Unify `..` notation and CLI across all subcommands
   423→
   424→- Extract `collect_ids` in `common.rs` — returns `(Vec<CommitId>, usize)`,
   425→  ordered commit IDs and anchor index. Handles all `..` directions.
   426→- Extract `resolve_spec` — converts positional/flag args into `DotSpec`
   427→  (desc_count/anc_count) ready for `collect_ids`. Single code path for
   428→  all subcommands.
   429→- Replace `DotDirection` enum with `desc_count`/`anc_count` on `DotSpec`.
   430→- Remove `resolve_dot_args`, `ResolvedArgs`, `collect_both`/`collect_commit_lines`,
   431→  `show_both` — all replaced by `resolve_spec` + `collect_ids`.
   432→- All subcommands (chid, desc, list, show) now share one loop pattern.
   433→- `-r` flag supports `..` notation (e.g. `-r ..mpl -n 3`).
   434→- Rename `-l`/`--limit` to `-n`/`--commits` (`-l`/`--limit` kept as
   435→  hidden aliases). Positional `COMMITS` replaces `POS_COUNT`.
   436→- Count means total commits including anchor (e.g. `x.. 3` = x + 2
   437→  ancestors). Fixes off-by-one in descendants and bare-rev-with-count.
   438→- File list truncation: show first N files only. Skip truncation when
   439→  only 1 file would be omitted.
   440→- Raw jj revset syntax (`::`, `|`, `&`) no longer supported via `-r`;
   441→  use `..` notation instead.
   442→
   443→## Test dispersal and ochid list column
   444→
   445→### 0.20.1 — Disperse CLI parsing tests
   446→
   447→Move all CLI parsing tests from main.rs into per-subcommand test modules
   448→(chid.rs, desc.rs, list.rs, show.rs, finalize.rs). main.rs retains only
   449→the `unknown_command` test. Make `Commands` enum `pub(crate)` so submodule
   450→tests can match on it. Each module has a local `parse()` helper that
   451→returns the typed args struct directly, eliminating the `if let` boilerplate.
   452→
   453→### 0.21.0 — Show ochid in list output
   454→
   455→Replace commitID with ochid trailer value in `list` output. New column
   456→format: `chid  ochid  title` with 2-space gaps between columns. The ochid
   457→column is padded to `-w`/`--width` (default 21, fits `/.claude/` + 12-char
   458→changeID). Commits without an ochid trailer show a blank placeholder to
   459→keep columns aligned. Add `extract_ochid()` and `format_commit_with_ochid()`
   460→to common.rs. Also clean up CLI help: remove duplicate manual defaults from
   461→doc comments (clap shows `[default: ...]` automatically), use `default_value`
   462→for `--label`, and simplify `Header` enum from three variants to two
   463→(`Label(String)` and `None`).
   464→
   465→### 0.21.1 — Deduplicate common CLI flags
   466→
   467→Extract shared CLI args (pos_rev, pos_count, revision, repos, limit, label,
   468→no_label) into `CommonArgs` struct in common.rs using clap `#[command(flatten)]`.
   469→All four read-only subcommands (chid, desc, list, show) flatten it; list adds
   470→`width`, show adds `files`. Eliminates ~30 lines of duplicated arg definitions
   471→per subcommand. Tests updated to access fields via `.common`.
   472→
   473→### 0.22.0 — Add fix-ochid subcommand
   474→
   475→New `fix-ochid` subcommand to validate and fix ochid trailers in commit
   476→descriptions. Validates three properties: correct path prefix (derived from
   477→`--other-repo`), correct changeID length (`--id-len`, default 12), and that
   478→the ID resolves in the other repo. Supports `..` notation for revision ranges.
   479→Default is dry-run; `--no-dry-run` to actually write. `--fallback` provides a
   480→replacement value for IDs not found in the other repo (e.g. `/.claude/lost`
   481→for commits whose `.claude` history was lost to squashing). Optional `--title`
   482→to fix commit titles at the same time. Uses `jj describe --ignore-immutable`
   483→to rewrite descriptions.
   484→
   485→### 0.22.1 — Fix fix-ochid prefix bug
   486→
   487→fix-ochid computed the ochid prefix from the raw `--other-repo` CLI path
   488→(e.g. `../` produced `/../`). Now reads `workspace.path` from the other
   489→repo's `.vc-config.toml`, which is the authoritative source for the
   490→workspace-root-relative path. Added `toml_simple` module with `toml_load()`
   491→(returns `HashMap<String, String>`) and `toml_get()` for minimal TOML
   492→parsing — designed to be swapped for a real TOML crate later.
   493→
   494→### 0.23.0 — Add --add-missing to fix-ochid
   495→
   496→New `--add-missing` flag for `fix-ochid` that adds ochid trailers to commits
   497→that don't have one. Matches commits by title (exact) and committer timestamp
   498→(within 60 seconds). Only adds the trailer when exactly one match is found in
   499→the other repo — ambiguous or zero matches are skipped. Respects `--no-dry-run`.
   500→
   501→### 0.22.2 — Fix fix-ochid short ID extension
   502→
   503→fix-ochid could only truncate IDs to `--id-len`, not extend them. When the
   504→existing ochid had an 8-char ID and `id_len` was 12, the fix produced the
   505→same 8-char ID — `jj describe` saw no change and said "Nothing changed."
   506→Now resolves the short ID in the other repo to get the full change ID, then
   507→truncates to `id_len`. Added `resolved_id` parameter to
   508→`fix_ochid_in_description`. Also added notes update steps (6–8) to the
   509→pre-commit checklist in CLAUDE.md.
   510→
   511→## Git commit headers and jj change-id preservation
   512→
   513→### How jj stores change IDs in git
   514→
   515→jj stores the change ID as a non-standard git commit header called
   516→`change-id`. This is part of the raw commit object, visible with
   517→`git cat-file commit <hash>`:
   518→
   519→```
   520→$ git cat-file commit f22d6c45b9d9
   521→tree 55d1626ba2ad932a568fd669d18cc19eb38510fa
   522→parent 432b7494734a5bbe549cd796335d9e67756f07bc
   523→author Wink Saville <wink@saville.com> 1773950107 -0700
   524→committer Wink Saville <wink@saville.com> 1773950107 -0700
   525→change-id mkoooktqrrqpllrkwxvosunuywplqsyq
   526→
   527→Deduplicate common CLI flags with #[command(flatten)] (0.21.1)
   528→
   529→Extract CommonArgs struct in common.rs shared by chid, desc, list, show.
   530→List adds width, show adds files on top. Update tests to access via .common.
   531→
   532→ochid: /.claude/lwwsmvroqzly
   533→```
   534→
   535→The `change-id` header sits alongside standard git headers (tree, parent,
   536→author, committer). The value is the full reverse-hex encoding of the
   537→change ID — jj's short form (`mkoooktqrrqp`) is a 12-char prefix of
   538→the full value (`mkoooktqrrqpllrkwxvosunuywplqsyq`).
   539→
   540→### Preservation guarantees
   541→
   542→From the [jj Git compatibility docs](https://jj-vcs.github.io/jj/latest/git-compatibility/):
   543→
   544→> Change IDs are stored in git commit headers as reverse hex encodings.
   545→> This is a non-standard header and is not preserved by all git tooling.
   546→> For example, the header is preserved by a `git commit --amend`, but is
   547→> not preserved through a rebase operation. GitHub and other major forges
   548→> seem to preserve them for the most part.
   549→
   550→**What preserves change IDs:**
   551→- `git push` / `git fetch` / `git clone` — the header is part of the
   552→  commit object, transferred as-is
   553→- `git commit --amend` — preserves non-standard headers
   554→- GitHub / major forges — preserve them in storage and API
   555→
   556→**What can strip change IDs:**
   557→- `git rebase` — rewrites commits, drops non-standard headers
   558→- `git cherry-pick` — creates new commits without the header
   559→- GitHub merge-squash / rebase-merge — creates new commits server-side
   560→
   561→### Configuration
   562→
   563→Controlled by `git.write-change-id-header` (default `true` since jj 0.29.0).
   564→Verify with:
   565→
   566→```
   567→$ jj config get git.write-change-id-header
   568→true
   569→```
   570→
   571→### Implications for ochid cross-references
   572→
   573→Since our ochid trailers reference change IDs in the *other* repo, the
   574→cross-references are only as stable as the change-id headers in both repos.
   575→A regular `git push` to GitHub and `git clone` back will preserve them,
   576→but any git tooling that rewrites commits could break the chain.
   577→
```

Positional `COMMITS` replaces `POS_COUNT`.\n- Count means total commits including anchor (e.g. `x.. 3` = x + 2\n  ancestors). Fixes off-by-one in descendants and bare-rev-with-count.\n- File list truncation: show first N files only. Skip truncation when\n  only 1 file would be omitted.\n- Raw jj revset syntax (`::`, `|`, `&`) no longer supported via `-r`;\n  use `..` notation instead.\n\n## 0.20.0 — Multi-repo support\n\n(Documented in previous session; see todo.md for summary.)\n\n### 0.20.1 — Disperse CLI parsing tests\n\nMove all CLI parsing tests from main.rs into per-subcommand test modules\n(chid.rs, desc.rs, list.rs, show.rs, finalize.rs). main.rs retains only\nthe `unknown_command` test. Make `Commands` enum `pub(crate)` so submodule\ntests can match on it. Each module has a local `parse()` helper that\nreturns the typed args struct directly, eliminating the `if let` boilerplate.\n\n## 0.21.0 — Show ochid in list output\n\nReplace commitID with ochid trailer value in `list` output. New column\nformat: `chid  ochid  title` with 2-space gaps between columns. The ochid\ncolumn is padded to `-w`/`--width` (default 21, fits `/.claude/` + 12-char\nchangeID). Commits without an ochid trailer show a blank placeholder to\nkeep columns aligned. Add `extract_ochid()` and `format_commit_with_ochid()`\nto common.rs. Also clean up CLI help: remove duplicate manual defaults from\ndoc comments (clap shows `[default: ...]` automatically), use `default_value`\nfor `--label`, and simplify `Header` enum from three variants to two\n(`Label(String)` and `None`).\n\n### 0.21.1 — Deduplicate common CLI flags\n\nExtract shared CLI args (pos_rev, pos_count, revision, repos, limit, label,\nno_label) into `CommonArgs` struct in common.rs using clap `#[command(flatten)]`.\nAll four read-only subcommands (chid, desc, list, show) flatten it; list adds\n`width`, show adds `files`. Eliminates ~30 lines of duplicated arg definitions\nper subcommand. Tests updated to access fields via `.common`.\n\n## 0.22.0 — Add fix-ochid subcommand\n\nNew `fix-ochid` subcommand to validate and fix ochid trailers in commit\ndescriptions. Validates three properties: correct path prefix (derived from\n`--other-repo`), correct changeID length (`--id-len`, default 12), and that\nthe ID resolves in the other repo. Supports `..` notation for revision ranges.\nDefault is dry-run; `--no-dry-run` to actually write. `--fallback` provides a\nreplacement value for IDs not found in the other repo (e.g. `/.claude/lost`\nfor commits whose `.claude` history was lost to squashing). Optional `--title`\nto fix commit titles at the same time. Uses `jj describe --ignore-immutable`\nto rewrite descriptions.\n\n### 0.22.1 — Fix fix-ochid prefix bug\n\nfix-ochid computed the ochid prefix from the raw `--other-repo` CLI path\n(e.g. `../` produced `/../`). Now reads `workspace.path` from the other\nrepo's `.vc-config.toml`, which is the authoritative source for the\nworkspace-root-relative path. Added `toml_simple` module with `toml_load()`\n(returns `HashMap<String, String>`) and `toml_get()` for minimal TOML\nparsing — designed to be swapped for a real TOML crate later.\n\n### 0.22.2 — Fix fix-ochid short ID extension\n\nfix-ochid could only truncate IDs to `--id-len`, not extend them. When the\nexisting ochid had an 8-char ID and `id_len` was 12, the fix produced the\nsame 8-char ID — `jj describe` saw no change and said \"Nothing changed.\"\nNow resolves the short ID in the other repo to get the full change ID, then\ntruncates to `id_len`. Added `resolved_id` parameter to\n`fix_ochid_in_description`. Also added notes update steps (6–8) to the\npre-commit checklist in CLAUDE.md.\n\n## 0.23.0 — Add --add-missing to fix-ochid\n\nNew `--add-missing` flag for `fix-ochid` that adds ochid trailers to commits\nthat don't have one. Matches commits by title (exact) and committer timestamp\n(within 60 seconds). Only adds the trailer when exactly one match is found in\nthe other repo — ambiguous or zero matches are skipped. Respects `--no-dry-run`."
}
```

> AGENT

Clap already shows `--add-missing` without a default (it's a bool flag, so implicitly optional/false). The `-n` has no default shown — which means it's `None`, so all matched commits are processed. That seems right for fix-ochid since you typically want to scan everything.

Do you want me to change anything here, or does the help look correct to you?

> AGENT

So `-n` limits how many commits are *collected* (scanned), not how many are *fixed*. With `-r @.. -n 5` you'd scan @ plus 4 ancestors. There's no "fix at most N" limit.

Your intuition about `-r @.. -n 1` is wrong given the current code — it would scan only @ (1 commit), not "scan all but fix 1."

So actually `-n` for fix-ochid works the same as for `list`/`desc`/`show` — it controls the scan window. The help text is just misleading. I'd suggest:

- `-r` help: "Revision to scan" (not "fix")  
- `-n` help: "Number of commits to scan"

Does that match your understanding, or do you actually want a separate "max fixes" limit?

Fix ochid trailers in commit descriptions

Usage: vc-x1 fix-ochid [OPTIONS] --other-repo <OTHER_REPO> [REVISION] [COMMITS]

Arguments:
  [REVISION]  Revision (with optional .. notation)
  [COMMITS]   Number of commits (per dotted side)

Options:
  -r, --revision <REVISION>      Revision(s) to scan [default: @]
  -R, --repo <REPO>              Path to jj repo [default: .]
      --other-repo <OTHER_REPO>  Path to the other repo (e.g. .claude or .)
      --id-len <ID_LEN>          Expected changeID length [default: 12]
      --title <TITLE>            New title to replace the first line (optional)
      --fallback <FALLBACK>      Fallback ochid value for IDs not found in other repo (e.g. /.claude/lost)
      --no-dry-run               Actually write changes (default is dry-run)
      --add-missing              Add missing ochid trailers by matching title and timestamp
      --max-fixes <MAX_FIXES>    Maximum number of commits to actually fix/change
  -n, --commits <COMMITS>        Number of commits to scan
  -h, --help                     Print help
```

Discuss the new todo

> AGENT

I see you added:

> Refactor fix-ochid into validate-desc fix-desc so something like that

Makes sense — `fix-ochid` has grown beyond just ochid trailers. It already fixes titles (`--title`), and the `--add-missing` feature is really about matching commits across repos. The name "fix-ochid" undersells what it does.

A split like:
- **`validate-desc`** — scan and report issues (wrong prefix, wrong length, missing trailer, etc.) — pure read-only
- **`fix-desc`** — actually rewrite descriptions (fix ochid, add missing, replace title)

Or maybe keep it as one command with a better name like `desc-fix` or `fix-desc` that signals it's about descriptions generally, not just ochid?

What's your thinking on the split vs rename?

> DEVELOPER

I think spliting but fix-desc still requires --no-dry-run!

> AGENT

Agreed — `fix-desc` keeps dry-run as default, `--no-dry-run` to write. The split just separates the "what's wrong" reporting from the "fix it" action, which is cleaner. But both are really about descriptions, not just ochid.

Good todo item for a future session.

> DEVELOPER

do it

> AGENT

Before I start — a couple of questions:

1. **Scope of validate-desc**: Just report issues (no `--no-dry-run` flag at all), essentially what `fix-ochid` does today without `--no-dry-run`?
2. **Scope of fix-desc**: Same as today's `fix-ochid` with `--no-dry-run` (dry-run default, `--no-dry-run` to write)?
3. **Remove fix-ochid** or keep as alias?
4. Version 0.25.0, single-step?

> DEVELOPER

1: y
2: y
3: remove
4: Mult-step with verify-desc first and refactoring into probably several helper functions that fix-desc would use, and maybe some of the helper fn's would go to common?

- **0.25.0-dev2**: Add `fix-desc` using the shared helpers
- **0.25.0-dev3**: Remove `fix-ochid`, final cleanup
- **0.25.0**: Remove `-devN`

Does that breakdown look right, or would you slice it differently?

     1→use std::path::PathBuf;

    10→use crate::common;

    14→const VC_CONFIG_FILE: &str = ".vc-config.toml";
    15→
    16→#[derive(Args, Debug)]

    48→    pub id_len: usize,

    51→    #[arg(long)]
    52→    pub title: Option<String>,
    53→
    54→    /// Fallback ochid value for IDs not found in other repo (e.g. /.claude/lost)
    55→    #[arg(long)]
    56→    pub fallback: Option<String>,
    57→
    58→    /// Actually write changes (default is dry-run)
    59→    #[arg(long = "no-dry-run")]
    60→    pub no_dry_run: bool,
    61→
    62→    /// Add missing ochid trailers by matching title and timestamp
    63→    #[arg(long = "add-missing")]
    64→    pub add_missing: bool,
    65→}
    66→
    67→/// Derive the ochid prefix from the other repo's `.vc-config.toml`.
    68→///
    69→/// The `workspace.path` value is the repo's path relative to the workspace
    70→/// root, which is exactly the ochid prefix (with a trailing `/` when the
    71→/// path isn't just `/`).
    72→fn ochid_prefix_from_config(
    73→    config: &std::collections::HashMap<String, String>,
    74→) -> Result<String, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    75→    let ws_path = toml_simple::toml_get(config, "workspace.path")
    76→        .ok_or("missing workspace.path in .vc-config.toml")?;
    77→    let trimmed = ws_path.trim_end_matches('/');
    78→    if trimmed.is_empty() {
    79→        Ok("/".to_string())
    80→    } else {
    81→        Ok(format!("{trimmed}/"))
    82→    }
    83→}
    84→
    85→/// Problems found with an ochid trailer.
    86→#[derive(Debug)]
    87→struct OchidIssues {
    88→    wrong_prefix: bool,
    89→    wrong_length: bool,
    90→    not_found: bool,
    91→}
    92→
    93→impl OchidIssues {
    94→    fn any(&self) -> bool {
    95→        self.wrong_prefix || self.wrong_length || self.not_found
    96→    }
    97→
    98→    fn summary(&self) -> String {
    99→        let mut parts = Vec::new();
   100→        if self.wrong_prefix {
   101→            parts.push("wrong prefix");
   102→        }
   103→        if self.wrong_length {
   104→            parts.push("wrong ID length");
   105→        }
   106→        if self.not_found {
   107→            parts.push("ID not found in other repo");
   108→        }
   109→        parts.join(", ")
   110→    }
   111→}
   112→
   113→/// Validate an ochid trailer value against expected prefix, length, and other repo.
   114→fn validate_ochid(
   115→    value: &str,
   116→    other_prefix: &str,
   117→    id_len: usize,
   118→    other_workspace: &Workspace,
   119→    other_repo: &Arc<ReadonlyRepo>,
   120→) -> OchidIssues {
   121→    let wrong_prefix = !value.starts_with(other_prefix);
   122→
   123→    // Extract the bare ID
   124→    let bare_id = if let Some(pos) = value.rfind('/') {
   125→        &value[pos + 1..]
   126→    } else {
   127→        value
   128→    };
   129→
   130→    let wrong_length = bare_id.len() != id_len;
   131→
   132→    // Check if the ID resolves in the other repo
   133→    let not_found = common::resolve_revset(other_workspace, other_repo, bare_id)
   134→        .map(|ids| ids.is_empty())
   135→        .unwrap_or(true);
   136→
   137→    OchidIssues {
   138→        wrong_prefix,
   139→        wrong_length,
   140→        not_found,
   141→    }
   142→}
   143→
   144→/// Fix the ochid trailer in a commit description.
   145→///
   146→/// If `resolved_id` is provided, it replaces the bare changeID entirely
   147→/// (used when the existing ID is too short to extend by truncation alone).
   148→fn fix_ochid_in_description(
   149→    desc: &str,
   150→    other_prefix: &str,
   151→    id_len: usize,
   152→    new_title: Option<&str>,
   153→    resolved_id: Option<&str>,
   154→) -> String {
   155→    let mut lines: Vec<String> = desc.lines().map(|l| l.to_string()).collect();
   156→
   157→    // Fix title if requested
   158→    if let Some(title) = new_title
   159→        && !lines.is_empty()
   160→    {
   161→        lines[0] = title.to_string();
   162→    }
   163→
   164→    // Fix ochid trailer
   165→    for line in &mut lines {
   166→        let trimmed = line.trim();
   167→        if let Some(value) = trimmed.strip_prefix("ochid:") {
   168→            let value = value.trim();
   169→            let id = if let Some(rid) = resolved_id {
   170→                &rid[..rid.len().min(id_len)]
   171→            } else {
   172→                // Extract the bare changeID (strip any existing path prefix)
   173→                let bare_id = if let Some(pos) = value.rfind('/') {
   174→                    &value[pos + 1..]
   175→                } else {
   176→                    value
   177→                };
   178→                // Normalize to id_len chars
   179→                &bare_id[..bare_id.len().min(id_len)]
   180→            };
   181→            *line = format!("ochid: {other_prefix}{id}");
   182→        }
   183→    }
   184→
   185→    // Preserve trailing newline if original had one
   186→    let mut result = lines.join("\n");
   187→    if desc.ends_with('\n') {
   188→        result.push('\n');
   189→    }
   190→    result
   191→}
   192→
   193→/// Maximum timestamp difference (in milliseconds) for title-based matching.
   194→const TIMESTAMP_TOLERANCE_MS: i64 = 60_000;
   195→
   196→/// Find a unique matching commit in the other repo by title and timestamp.
   197→///
   198→/// Returns the other commit's change ID (full hex) if exactly one commit
   199→/// in the other repo has the same first description line and a committer
   200→/// timestamp within `TIMESTAMP_TOLERANCE_MS` of the source commit.
   201→fn find_matching_commit(
   202→    commit: &jj_lib::commit::Commit,
   203→    other_workspace: &Workspace,
   204→    other_repo: &Arc<ReadonlyRepo>,
   205→) -> Result<Option<String>, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
   206→    let title = commit.description().lines().next().unwrap_or("");
   207→    if title.is_empty() {
   208→        return Ok(None);
   209→    }
   210→    let src_millis = commit.committer().timestamp.timestamp.0;
   211→
   212→    // Search all commits in the other repo
   213→    let all_ids = common::resolve_revset(other_workspace, other_repo, "all()")?;
   214→    let root_id = other_repo.store().root_commit_id().clone();
   215→
   216→    let mut matches = Vec::new();
   217→    for cid in &all_ids {
   218→        if *cid == root_id {
   219→            continue;
   220→        }
   221→        let other_commit = other_repo.store().get_commit(cid)?;
   222→        let other_title = other_commit.description().lines().next().unwrap_or("");
   223→        if other_title != title {
   224→            continue;
   225→        }
   226→        let other_millis = other_commit.committer().timestamp.timestamp.0;
   227→        if (src_millis - other_millis).abs() <= TIMESTAMP_TOLERANCE_MS {
   228→            let full_hex =
   229→                jj_lib::hex_util::encode_reverse_hex(other_commit.change_id().as_bytes());
   230→            matches.push(full_hex);
   231→        }
   232→    }
   233→
   234→    if matches.len() == 1 {
   235→        Ok(Some(matches.into_iter().next().unwrap()))
   236→    } else {
   237→        Ok(None)
   238→    }
   239→}
   240→
   241→/// Append an ochid trailer to a commit description.
   242→fn append_ochid_trailer(desc: &str, other_prefix: &str, change_id: &str, id_len: usize) -> String {
   243→    let short_id = &change_id[..change_id.len().min(id_len)];
   244→    let trailer = format!("ochid: {other_prefix}{short_id}");
   245→
   246→    let mut result = desc.trim_end().to_string();
   247→    // Add blank line before trailer if body exists, otherwise just newline
   248→    if result.lines().count() > 1 {
   249→        result.push('\n');
   250→    } else {
   251→        result.push_str("\n\n");
   252→    }
   253→    result.push_str(&trailer);
   254→    result.push('\n');
   255→    result
   256→}
   257→
   258→pub fn fix_ochid(args: &FixOchidArgs) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
   259→    let (workspace, repo) = common::load_repo(&args.repo)?;
   260→    let (other_workspace, other_repo) = common::load_repo(&args.other_repo)?;
   261→    let other_config = toml_simple::toml_load(&args.other_repo.join(VC_CONFIG_FILE))?;
   262→    let other_prefix = ochid_prefix_from_config(&other_config)?;
   263→
   264→    let spec = common::resolve_spec(
   265→        args.pos_rev.as_deref(),
   266→        args.pos_count,
   267→        &args.revision,
   268→        args.limit,
   269→        "@",
   270→    );
   271→
   272→    let (ids, _anchor_index) = common::collect_ids(
   273→        &workspace,
   274→        &repo,
   275→        &spec.rev,
   276→        spec.desc_count,
   277→        spec.anc_count,
   278→    )?;
   279→    if ids.is_empty() {
   280→        return Err(format!("no commits found for revision '{}'", args.revision).into());
   281→    }
   282→
   283→    let root_id = repo.store().root_commit_id().clone();
   284→    let mut fixed = 0;
   285→    let mut skipped = 0;
   286→    let mut errors = 0;
   287→
   288→    for commit_id in &ids {
   289→        if *commit_id == root_id {
   290→            continue;
   291→        }
   292→        let commit = repo.store().get_commit(commit_id)?;
   293→        let desc = commit.description();
   294→        let change_hex = jj_lib::hex_util::encode_reverse_hex(commit.change_id().as_bytes());
   295→        let change_short = &change_hex[..change_hex.len().min(12)];
   296→
   297→        // Extract current ochid value
   298→        let current_ochid = common::extract_ochid(&commit);
   299→
   300→        // Validate current ochid
   301→        let issues = if let Some(ref ochid_val) = current_ochid {
   302→            validate_ochid(
   303→                ochid_val,
   304→                &other_prefix,
   305→                args.id_len,
   306→                &other_workspace,
   307→                &other_repo,
   308→            )
   309→        } else if args.add_missing {
   310→            // Stop if we've hit the max-fixes limit
   311→            if let Some(max) = args.max_fixes
   312→                && fixed >= max
   313→            {
   314→                skipped += 1;
   315→                if !args.no_dry_run {
   316→                    println!("skip {change_short}  (max-fixes reached)");
   317→                }
   318→                continue;
   319→            }
   320→            // No ochid trailer — try to infer from the other repo
   321→            if let Some(matched_id) = find_matching_commit(&commit, &other_workspace, &other_repo)?
   322→            {
   323→                let new_desc = append_ochid_trailer(desc, &other_prefix, &matched_id, args.id_len);
   324→                let first_line = desc.lines().next().unwrap_or("");
   325→                let short_matched = &matched_id[..matched_id.len().min(args.id_len)];
   326→                if !args.no_dry_run {
   327→                    println!("add  {change_short}  {first_line}  [missing]");
   328→                    println!("     -> ochid: {other_prefix}{short_matched}");
   329→                } else {
   330→                    let status = Command::new("jj")
   331→                        .arg("describe")
   332→                        .arg("-m")
   333→                        .arg(&new_desc)
   334→                        .arg("-r")
   335→                        .arg(commit_id.hex())
   336→                        .arg("-R")
   337→                        .arg(&args.repo)
   338→                        .arg("--ignore-immutable")
   339→                        .stdout(std::process::Stdio::null())
   340→                        .stderr(std::process::Stdio::inherit())
   341→                        .status()?;
   342→                    if !status.success() {
   343→                        return Err(format!(
   344→                            "jj describe failed for {} (exit {})",
   345→                            change_short,
   346→                            status.code().unwrap_or(-1)
   347→                        )
   348→                        .into());
   349→                    }
   350→                    println!("added {change_short}  {first_line}");
   351→                }
   352→                fixed += 1;
   353→                continue;
   354→            } else {
   355→                skipped += 1;
   356→                if !args.no_dry_run {
   357→                    println!("skip {change_short}  (no ochid, no match in other repo)");
   358→                }
   359→                continue;
   360→            }
   361→        } else {
   362→            // No ochid trailer — nothing to fix
   363→            skipped += 1;
   364→            if !args.no_dry_run {
   365→                println!("skip {change_short}  (no ochid trailer)");
   366→            }
   367→            continue;
   368→        };
   369→
   370→        if !issues.any() {
   371→            skipped += 1;
   372→            if !args.no_dry_run {
   373→                println!("ok   {change_short}  (valid)");
   374→            }
   375→            continue;
   376→        }
   377→
   378→        // Stop if we've hit the max-fixes limit
   379→        if let Some(max) = args.max_fixes
   380→            && fixed >= max
   381→        {
   382→            skipped += 1;
   383→            if !args.no_dry_run {
   384→                println!("skip {change_short}  (max-fixes reached)");
   385→            }
   386→            continue;
   387→        }
   388→
   389→        // Resolve the full change ID from the other repo when length is wrong
   390→        let resolved_id = if issues.wrong_length {
   391→            let ochid_val = current_ochid.as_deref().unwrap_or("");
   392→            let bare_id = if let Some(pos) = ochid_val.rfind('/') {
   393→                &ochid_val[pos + 1..]
   394→            } else {
   395→                ochid_val
   396→            };
   397→            if let Ok(commit_ids) = common::resolve_revset(&other_workspace, &other_repo, bare_id)
   398→                && let Some(cid) = commit_ids.first()
   399→            {
   400→                let other_commit = other_repo.store().get_commit(cid)?;
   401→                let full_hex =
   402→                    jj_lib::hex_util::encode_reverse_hex(other_commit.change_id().as_bytes());
   403→                Some(full_hex)
   404→            } else {
   405→                None
   406→            }
   407→        } else {
   408→            None
   409→        };
   410→
   411→        // Build the fixed description
   412→        let mut new_desc = fix_ochid_in_description(
   413→            desc,
   414→            &other_prefix,
   415→            args.id_len,
   416→            args.title.as_deref(),
   417→            resolved_id.as_deref(),
   418→        );
   419→
   420→        // Re-extract the fixed ochid value for post-validation
   421→        let mut fixed_ochid = {
   422→            let mut val = None;
   423→            for line in new_desc.lines() {
   424→                let trimmed = line.trim();
   425→                if let Some(v) = trimmed.strip_prefix("ochid:") {
   426→                    val = Some(v.trim().to_string());
   427→                }
   428→            }
   429→            val
   430→        };
   431→
   432→        let post_issues = if let Some(ref v) = fixed_ochid {
   433→            validate_ochid(v, &other_prefix, args.id_len, &other_workspace, &other_repo)
   434→        } else {
   435→            OchidIssues {
   436→                wrong_prefix: false,
   437→                wrong_length: false,
   438→                not_found: true,
   439→            }
   440→        };
   441→
   442→        if post_issues.not_found {
   443→            if let Some(ref fallback) = args.fallback {
   444→                // Replace the ochid line with the fallback value
   445→                let mut fb_lines: Vec<String> = new_desc.lines().map(|l| l.to_string()).collect();
   446→                for line in &mut fb_lines {
   447→                    if line.trim().starts_with("ochid:") {
   448→                        *line = format!("ochid: {fallback}");
   449→                    }
   450→                }
   451→                let fb_desc = if new_desc.ends_with('\n') {
   452→                    format!("{}\n", fb_lines.join("\n"))
   453→                } else {
   454→                    fb_lines.join("\n")
   455→                };
   456→                new_desc = fb_desc;
   457→                fixed_ochid = Some(fallback.clone());
   458→            } else {
   459→                errors += 1;
   460→                eprintln!(
   461→                    "err  {change_short}  ID not found in other repo (ochid: {})",
   462→                    fixed_ochid.as_deref().unwrap_or("?")
   463→                );
   464→                continue;
   465→            }
   466→        }
   467→
   468→        if !args.no_dry_run {
   469→            let first_line = new_desc.lines().next().unwrap_or("");
   470→            println!("fix  {change_short}  {first_line}  [{}]", issues.summary());
   471→            if let Some(ref v) = fixed_ochid {
   472→                println!("     -> ochid: {v}");
   473→            }
   474→        } else {
   475→            let status = Command::new("jj")
   476→                .arg("describe")
   477→                .arg("-m")
   478→                .arg(&new_desc)
   479→                .arg("-r")
   480→                .arg(commit_id.hex())
   481→                .arg("-R")
   482→                .arg(&args.repo)
   483→                .arg("--ignore-immutable")
   484→                .stdout(std::process::Stdio::null())
   485→                .stderr(std::process::Stdio::inherit())
   486→                .status()?;
   487→            if !status.success() {
   488→                return Err(format!(
   489→                    "jj describe failed for {} (exit {})",
   490→                    change_short,
   491→                    status.code().unwrap_or(-1)
   492→                )
   493→                .into());
   494→            }
   495→            let first_line = new_desc.lines().next().unwrap_or("");
   496→            println!("fixed {change_short}  {first_line}");
   497→        }
   498→        fixed += 1;
   499→    }
   500→
   501→    println!(
   502→        "\n{fixed} fixed, {skipped} skipped, {errors} errors (of {} total)",
   503→        ids.len()
   504→    );
   505→    if errors > 0 {
   506→        Err(format!("{errors} commit(s) could not be fixed").into())
   507→    } else {
   508→        Ok(())
   509→    }
   510→}

difference (in milliseconds) for title-based matching.\npub const TIMESTAMP_TOLERANCE_MS: i64 = 60_000;\n\n/// Derive the ochid prefix from a repo's `.vc-config.toml`.\n///\n/// The `workspace.path` value is the repo's path relative to the workspace\n/// root, which is exactly the ochid prefix (with a trailing `/` when the\n/// path isn't just `/`).\npub fn ochid_prefix_from_config(\n    config: &HashMap<String, String>,\n) -> Result<String, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let ws_path = toml_simple::toml_get(config, \"workspace.path\")\n        .ok_or(\"missing workspace.path in .vc-config.toml\")?;\n    let trimmed = ws_path.trim_end_matches('/');\n    if trimmed.is_empty() {\n        Ok(\"/\".to_string())\n    } else {\n        Ok(format!(\"{trimmed}/\"))\n    }\n}\n\n/// Problems found with an ochid trailer.\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub struct OchidIssues {\n    pub wrong_prefix: bool,\n    pub wrong_length: bool,\n    pub not_found: bool,\n}\n\nimpl OchidIssues {\n    pub fn any(&self) -> bool {\n        self.wrong_prefix || self.wrong_length || self.not_found\n    }\n\n    pub fn summary(&self) -> String {\n        let mut parts = Vec::new();\n        if self.wrong_prefix {\n            parts.push(\"wrong prefix\");\n        }\n        if self.wrong_length {\n            parts.push(\"wrong ID length\");\n        }\n        if self.not_found {\n            parts.push(\"ID not found in other repo\");\n        }\n        parts.join(\", \")\n    }\n}\n\n/// Validate an ochid trailer value against expected prefix, length, and other repo.\npub fn validate_ochid(\n    value: &str,\n    other_prefix: &str,\n    id_len: usize,\n    other_workspace: &Workspace,\n    other_repo: &Arc<ReadonlyRepo>,\n) -> OchidIssues {\n    let wrong_prefix = !value.starts_with(other_prefix);\n\n    // Extract the bare ID\n    let bare_id = if let Some(pos) = value.rfind('/') {\n        &value[pos + 1..]\n    } else {\n        value\n    };\n\n    let wrong_length = bare_id.len() != id_len;\n\n    // Check if the ID resolves in the other repo\n    let not_found = common::resolve_revset(other_workspace, other_repo, bare_id)\n        .map(|ids| ids.is_empty())\n        .unwrap_or(true);\n\n    OchidIssues {\n        wrong_prefix,\n        wrong_length,\n        not_found,\n    }\n}\n\n/// Find a unique matching commit in the other repo by title and timestamp.\n///\n/// Returns the other commit's change ID (full hex) if exactly one commit\n/// in the other repo has the same first description line and a committer\n/// timestamp within `TIMESTAMP_TOLERANCE_MS` of the source commit.\npub fn find_matching_commit(\n    commit: &Commit,\n    other_workspace: &Workspace,\n    other_repo: &Arc<ReadonlyRepo>,\n) -> Result<Option<String>, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let title = commit.description().lines().next().unwrap_or(\"\");\n    if title.is_empty() {\n        return Ok(None);\n    }\n    let src_millis = commit.committer().timestamp.timestamp.0;\n\n    // Search all commits in the other repo\n    let all_ids = common::resolve_revset(other_workspace, other_repo, \"all()\")?;\n    let root_id = other_repo.store().root_commit_id().clone();\n\n    let mut matches = Vec::new();\n    for cid in &all_ids {\n        if *cid == root_id {\n            continue;\n        }\n        let other_commit = other_repo.store().get_commit(cid)?;\n        let other_title = other_commit.description().lines().next().unwrap_or(\"\");\n        if other_title != title {\n            continue;\n        }\n        let other_millis = other_commit.committer().timestamp.timestamp.0;\n        if (src_millis - other_millis).abs() <= TIMESTAMP_TOLERANCE_MS {\n            let full_hex =\n                jj_lib::hex_util::encode_reverse_hex(other_commit.change_id().as_bytes());\n            matches.push(full_hex);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if matches.len() == 1 {\n        Ok(Some(matches.into_iter().next().unwrap()))\n    } else {\n        Ok(None)\n    }\n}\n\n/// Resolve a short change ID to its full hex via the other repo.\npub fn resolve_full_change_id(\n    bare_id: &str,\n    other_workspace: &Workspace,\n    other_repo: &Arc<ReadonlyRepo>,\n) -> Result<Option<String>, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    if let Ok(commit_ids) = common::resolve_revset(other_workspace, other_repo, bare_id)\n        && let Some(cid) = commit_ids.first()\n    {\n        let other_commit = other_repo.store().get_commit(cid)?;\n        let full_hex = jj_lib::hex_util::encode_reverse_hex(other_commit.change_id().as_bytes());\n        Ok(Some(full_hex))\n    } else {\n        Ok(None)\n    }\n}\n\n/// Extract the bare change ID from an ochid value (strip path prefix).\npub fn extract_bare_id(ochid_value: &str) -> &str {\n    if let Some(pos) = ochid_value.rfind('/') {\n        &ochid_value[pos + 1..]\n    } else {\n        ochid_value\n    }\n}\n\n/// Fix the ochid trailer in a commit description.\n///\n/// If `resolved_id` is provided, it replaces the bare changeID entirely\n/// (used when the existing ID is too short to extend by truncation alone).\npub fn fix_ochid_in_description(\n    desc: &str,\n    other_prefix: &str,\n    id_len: usize,\n    new_title: Option<&str>,\n    resolved_id: Option<&str>,\n) -> String {\n    let mut lines: Vec<String> = desc.lines().map(|l| l.to_string()).collect();\n\n    // Fix title if requested\n    if let Some(title) = new_title\n        && !lines.is_empty()\n    {\n        lines[0] = title.to_string();\n    }\n\n    // Fix ochid trailer\n    for line in &mut lines {\n        let trimmed = line.trim();\n        if let Some(value) = trimmed.strip_prefix(\"ochid:\") {\n            let value = value.trim();\n            let id = if let Some(rid) = resolved_id {\n                &rid[..rid.len().min(id_len)]\n            } else {\n                let bare_id = extract_bare_id(value);\n                &bare_id[..bare_id.len().min(id_len)]\n            };\n            *line = format!(\"ochid: {other_prefix}{id}\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Preserve trailing newline if original had one\n    let mut result = lines.join(\"\\n\");\n    if desc.ends_with('\\n') {\n        result.push('\\n');\n    }\n    result\n}\n\n/// Append an ochid trailer to a commit description.\npub fn append_ochid_trailer(desc: &str, other_prefix: &str, change_id: &str, id_len: usize) -> String {\n    let short_id = &change_id[..change_id.len().min(id_len)];\n    let trailer = format!(\"ochid: {other_prefix}{short_id}\");\n\n    let mut result = desc.trim_end().to_string();\n    // Add blank line before trailer if body exists, otherwise just newline\n    if result.lines().count() > 1 {\n        result.push('\\n');\n    } else {\n        result.push_str(\"\\n\\n\");\n    }\n    result.push_str(&trailer);\n    result.push('\\n');\n    result\n}\n\n/// Extract the ochid value from a description string (without needing a Commit).\npub fn extract_ochid_from_desc(desc: &str) -> Option<String> {\n    for line in desc.lines() {\n        let trimmed = line.trim();\n        if let Some(v) = trimmed.strip_prefix(\"ochid:\") {\n            return Some(v.trim().to_string());\n        }\n    }\n    None\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n\n    fn make_config(ws_path: &str) -> HashMap<String, String> {\n        let mut map = HashMap::new();\n        map.insert(\"workspace.path\".to_string(), ws_path.to_string());\n        map\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn prefix_from_root() {\n        let config = make_config(\"/\");\n        assert_eq!(ochid_prefix_from_config(&config).unwrap(), \"/\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn prefix_from_claude() {\n        let config = make_config(\"/.claude\");\n        assert_eq!(ochid_prefix_from_config(&config).unwrap(), \"/.claude/\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn prefix_from_nested_path() {\n        let config = make_config(\"/some/path\");\n        assert_eq!(ochid_prefix_from_config(&config).unwrap(), \"/some/path/\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn prefix_missing_config_key() {\n        let config = HashMap::new();\n        assert!(ochid_prefix_from_config(&config).is_err());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn fix_bare_id() {\n        let desc = \"Some title\\n\\nBody text.\\n\\nochid: tzupykyyvnrp\\n\";\n        let result = fix_ochid_in_description(desc, \"/.claude/\", DEFAULT_ID_LEN, None, None);\n        assert!(result.contains(\"ochid: /.claude/tzupykyyvnrp\"));\n        assert!(result.starts_with(\"Some title\\n\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn fix_wrong_prefix() {\n        let desc = \"Title\\n\\nochid: /wrong/tzupykyyvnrp\\n\";\n        let result = fix_ochid_in_description(desc, \"/.claude/\", DEFAULT_ID_LEN, None, None);\n        assert!(result.contains(\"ochid: /.claude/tzupykyyvnrp\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn already_correct() {\n        let desc = \"Title\\n\\nochid: /.claude/tzupykyyvnrp\\n\";\n        let result = fix_ochid_in_description(desc, \"/.claude/\", DEFAULT_ID_LEN, None, None);\n        assert_eq!(result, desc);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn truncate_long_id() {\n        let desc = \"Title\\n\\nochid: abcdefghijklmnop\\n\";\n        let result = fix_ochid_in_description(desc, \"/\", DEFAULT_ID_LEN, None, None);\n        assert!(result.contains(\"ochid: /abcdefghijkl\"));\n        assert!(!result.contains(\"mnop\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn fix_title_and_ochid() {\n        let desc = \"Old title\\n\\nochid: bare12345678\\n\";\n        let result =\n            fix_ochid_in_description(desc, \"/.claude/\", DEFAULT_ID_LEN, Some(\"New title\"), None);\n        assert!(result.starts_with(\"New title\\n\"));\n        assert!(result.contains(\"ochid: /.claude/bare12345678\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn no_ochid_no_change() {\n        let desc = \"Title\\n\\nNo trailer here.\\n\";\n        let result = fix_ochid_in_description(desc, \"/.claude/\", DEFAULT_ID_LEN, None, None);\n        assert_eq!(result, desc);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn other_path_without_trailing_slash() {\n        let desc = \"Title\\n\\nochid: abcdefghijkl\\n\";\n        let result = fix_ochid_in_description(desc, \"/\", DEFAULT_ID_LEN, None, None);\n        assert!(result.contains(\"ochid: /abcdefghijkl\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn custom_id_len() {\n        let desc = \"Title\\n\\nochid: abcdefghijklmnop\\n\";\n        let result = fix_ochid_in_description(desc, \"/\", 8, None, None);\n        assert!(result.contains(\"ochid: /abcdefgh\"));\n        assert!(!result.contains(\"ijkl\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn extend_short_id_with_resolved() {\n        let desc = \"Title\\n\\nochid: /abcdefgh\\n\";\n        let result =\n            fix_ochid_in_description(desc, \"/\", DEFAULT_ID_LEN, None, Some(\"abcdefghijkl\"));\n        assert!(result.contains(\"ochid: /abcdefghijkl\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn resolved_id_with_prefix_fix() {\n        let desc = \"Title\\n\\nochid: /wrong/abcdefgh\\n\";\n        let result = fix_ochid_in_description(\n            desc,\n            \"/.claude/\",\n            DEFAULT_ID_LEN,\n            None,\n            Some(\"abcdefghijklmnop\"),\n        );\n        assert!(result.contains(\"ochid: /.claude/abcdefghijkl\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn append_ochid_title_only() {\n        let desc = \"Title\\n\";\n        let result = append_ochid_trailer(desc, \"/.claude/\", \"abcdefghijklmnop\", DEFAULT_ID_LEN);\n        assert_eq!(result, \"Title\\n\\nochid: /.claude/abcdefghijkl\\n\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn append_ochid_with_body() {\n        let desc = \"Title\\n\\nSome body text.\\n\";\n        let result = append_ochid_trailer(desc, \"/\", \"abcdefghijklmnop\", DEFAULT_ID_LEN);\n        assert_eq!(result, \"Title\\n\\nSome body text.\\nochid: /abcdefghijkl\\n\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn append_ochid_root_prefix() {\n        let desc = \"Title\\n\";\n        let result = append_ochid_trailer(desc, \"/\", \"xyzxyzxyzxyz\", DEFAULT_ID_LEN);\n        assert_eq!(result, \"Title\\n\\nochid: /xyzxyzxyzxyz\\n\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn extract_bare_id_with_prefix() {\n        assert_eq!(extract_bare_id(\"/.claude/abcdefghijkl\"), \"abcdefghijkl\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn extract_bare_id_root_prefix() {\n        assert_eq!(extract_bare_id(\"/abcdefghijkl\"), \"abcdefghijkl\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn extract_bare_id_no_prefix() {\n        assert_eq!(extract_bare_id(\"abcdefghijkl\"), \"abcdefghijkl\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn extract_ochid_from_desc_found() {\n        let desc = \"Title\\n\\nBody.\\n\\nochid: /.claude/abcdefghijkl\\n\";\n        assert_eq!(\n            extract_ochid_from_desc(desc),\n            Some(\"/.claude/abcdefghijkl\".to_string())\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn extract_ochid_from_desc_missing() {\n        let desc = \"Title\\n\\nNo trailer.\\n\";\n        assert_eq!(extract_ochid_from_desc(desc), None);\n    }\n}\n"
}

  "content": "use std::path::PathBuf;\nuse std::sync::Arc;\n\nuse clap::Args;\nuse jj_lib::object_id::ObjectId;\nuse jj_lib::repo::{ReadonlyRepo, Repo};\nuse jj_lib::workspace::Workspace;\n\nuse crate::common;\nuse crate::desc_helpers::{\n    self, find_matching_commit, ochid_prefix_from_config, validate_ochid, DEFAULT_ID_LEN,\n    VC_CONFIG_FILE,\n};\nuse crate::toml_simple;\n\n#[derive(Args, Debug)]\npub struct ValidateDescArgs {\n    /// Revision (with optional .. notation)\n    #[arg(value_name = \"REVISION\")]\n    pub pos_rev: Option<String>,\n\n    /// Number of commits (per dotted side)\n    #[arg(value_name = \"COMMITS\")]\n    pub pos_count: Option<usize>,\n\n    /// Revision(s) to scan\n    #[arg(short, long, default_value = \"@\")]\n    pub revision: String,\n\n    /// Number of commits to scan\n    #[arg(short = 'n', long = \"commits\", value_name = \"COMMITS\")]\n    pub limit: Option<usize>,\n\n    /// Path to jj repo\n    #[arg(short = 'R', long, default_value = \".\")]\n    pub repo: PathBuf,\n\n    /// Path to the other repo (e.g. .claude or .)\n    #[arg(long = \"other-repo\")]\n    pub other_repo: PathBuf,\n\n    /// Expected changeID length\n    #[arg(long = \"id-len\", default_value_t = DEFAULT_ID_LEN)]\n    pub id_len: usize,\n\n    /// Check for missing ochid trailers (report matches in other repo)\n    #[arg(long = \"check-missing\")]\n    pub check_missing: bool,\n}\n\n/// Status of a single commit's ochid validation.\nenum CommitStatus {\n    Ok,\n    NeedsFixed(String),    // summary of issues\n    Missing,               // no ochid trailer\n    MissingWithMatch(String), // no ochid, but match found in other repo\n    Skip(String),          // skipped with reason\n}\n\npub fn validate_desc(args: &ValidateDescArgs) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let (workspace, repo) = common::load_repo(&args.repo)?;\n    let (other_workspace, other_repo) = common::load_repo(&args.other_repo)?;\n    let other_config = toml_simple::toml_load(&args.other_repo.join(VC_CONFIG_FILE))?;\n    let other_prefix = ochid_prefix_from_config(&other_config)?;\n\n    let spec = common::resolve_spec(\n        args.pos_rev.as_deref(),\n        args.pos_count,\n        &args.revision,\n        args.limit,\n        \"@\",\n    );\n\n    let (ids, _anchor_index) = common::collect_ids(\n        &workspace,\n        &repo,\n        &spec.rev,\n        spec.desc_count,\n        spec.anc_count,\n    )?;\n    if ids.is_empty() {\n        return Err(format!(\"no commits found for revision '{}'\", args.revision).into());\n    }\n\n    let root_id = repo.store().root_commit_id().clone();\n    let mut valid = 0;\n    let mut issues_count = 0;\n    let mut missing = 0;\n    let mut skipped = 0;\n\n    for commit_id in &ids {\n        if *commit_id == root_id {\n            continue;\n        }\n        let commit = repo.store().get_commit(commit_id)?;\n        let desc = commit.description();\n        let change_hex = jj_lib::hex_util::encode_reverse_hex(commit.change_id().as_bytes());\n        let change_short = &change_hex[..change_hex.len().min(12)];\n        let first_line = desc.lines().next().unwrap_or(\"\");\n\n        let current_ochid = common::extract_ochid(&commit);\n\n        let status = if let Some(ref ochid_val) = current_ochid {\n            let issues = validate_ochid(\n                ochid_val,\n                &other_prefix,\n                args.id_len,\n                &other_workspace,\n                &other_repo,\n            );\n            if issues.any() {\n                CommitStatus::NeedsFixed(issues.summary())\n            } else {\n                CommitStatus::Ok\n            }\n        } else if args.check_missing {\n            if let Some(matched_id) =\n                find_matching_commit(&commit, &other_workspace, &other_repo)?\n            {\n                let short_id = &matched_id[..matched_id.len().min(args.id_len)];\n                CommitStatus::MissingWithMatch(format!(\"{other_prefix}{short_id}\"))\n            } else {\n                CommitStatus::Missing\n            }\n        } else {\n            CommitStatus::Skip(\"no ochid trailer\".to_string())\n        };\n\n        match status {\n            CommitStatus::Ok => {\n                valid += 1;\n                println!(\"ok   {change_short}  {first_line}\");\n            }\n            CommitStatus::NeedsFixed(summary) => {\n                issues_count += 1;\n                println!(\"err  {change_short}  {first_line}  [{summary}]\");\n            }\n            CommitStatus::MissingWithMatch(ochid) => {\n                missing += 1;\n                println!(\"miss {change_short}  {first_line}  [match: {ochid}]\");\n            }\n            CommitStatus::Missing => {\n                missing += 1;\n                println!(\"miss {change_short}  {first_line}  [no match in other repo]\");\n            }\n            CommitStatus::Skip(reason) => {\n                skipped += 1;\n                println!(\"skip {change_short}  ({reason})\");\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    println!(\n        \"\\n{valid} valid, {issues_count} issues, {missing} missing, {skipped} skipped (of {} total)\",\n        ids.len()\n    );\n    if issues_count > 0 {\n        Err(format!(\"{issues_count} commit(s) have issues\").into())\n    } else {\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n"
}

     1→mod chid;

    10→use std::process::ExitCode;
    11→
    12→use clap::{Parser, Subcommand};
    13→
    14→#[derive(Parser, Debug)]
    15→#[command(version, about = "vc-x1: jj workspace tooling")]
    16→pub struct Cli {
    17→    #[command(subcommand)]
    18→    pub(crate) command: Commands,
    19→}
    20→
    21→#[derive(Subcommand, Debug)]
    22→pub(crate) enum Commands {
    23→    /// Print the changeID for a revision
    24→    Chid(chid::ChidArgs),
    25→
    26→    /// Show full description of a commit
    27→    Desc(desc::DescArgs),
    28→
    29→    /// List commits in a jj repo
    30→    List(list::ListArgs),
    31→
    32→    /// Show commit details and diff summary
    33→    Show(show::ShowArgs),
    34→
    35→    /// Fix ochid trailers in commit descriptions
    36→    FixOchid(fix_ochid::FixOchidArgs),
    37→
    38→    /// Squash working copy into target commit
    39→    Finalize(finalize::FinalizeArgs),
    40→}
    41→
    42→fn main() -> ExitCode {
    43→    let cli = Cli::parse();
    44→
    45→    match cli.command {
    46→        Commands::Chid(args) => {
    47→            if let Err(e) = chid::chid(&args) {
    48→                eprintln!("error: {e}");
    49→                return ExitCode::FAILURE;
    50→            }
    51→            ExitCode::SUCCESS
    52→        }
    53→        Commands::Desc(args) => {
    54→            if let Err(e) = desc::desc(&args) {
    55→                eprintln!("error: {e}");
    56→                return ExitCode::FAILURE;
    57→            }
    58→            ExitCode::SUCCESS
    59→        }
    60→        Commands::List(args) => {
    61→            if let Err(e) = list::list(&args) {
    62→                eprintln!("error: {e}");
    63→                return ExitCode::FAILURE;
    64→            }
    65→            ExitCode::SUCCESS
    66→        }
    67→        Commands::Show(args) => {
    68→            if let Err(e) = show::show(&args) {
    69→                eprintln!("error: {e}");
    70→                return ExitCode::FAILURE;
    71→            }
    72→            ExitCode::SUCCESS
    73→        }
    74→        Commands::FixOchid(args) => {
    75→            if let Err(e) = fix_ochid::fix_ochid(&args) {
    76→                eprintln!("error: {e}");
    77→                return ExitCode::FAILURE;
    78→            }
    79→            ExitCode::SUCCESS
    80→        }
    81→        Commands::Finalize(args) => {
    82→            let opts = args.into_opts();
    83→            finalize::log_msg(&opts.log, "main: finalize entry");
    84→            match finalize::finalize(&opts) {
    85→                Ok(()) => {
    86→                    finalize::log_msg(&opts.log, "main: finalize exit ok");
    87→                    ExitCode::SUCCESS
    88→                }
    89→                Err(e) => {
    90→                    finalize::log_msg(&opts.log, &format!("main: finalize exit err={e}"));
    91→                    eprintln!("error: {e}");
    92→                    ExitCode::FAILURE
    93→                }
    94→            }
    95→        }
    96→    }
    97→}
    98→

   106→            .unwrap_err()
   107→            .to_string();
   108→        assert!(err.contains("bogus"));
   109→    }
