    13→const DEFAULT_ID_LEN: usize = 12;
    14→const VC_CONFIG_FILE: &str = ".vc-config.toml";
    15→
    16→#[derive(Args, Debug)]
    17→pub struct FixOchidArgs {
    18→    /// Revision (with optional .. notation)
    19→    #[arg(value_name = "REVISION")]
    20→    pub pos_rev: Option<String>,
    21→
    22→    /// Number of commits (per dotted side)
    23→    #[arg(value_name = "COMMITS")]
    24→    pub pos_count: Option<usize>,
    25→
    26→    /// Revision(s) to scan
    27→    #[arg(short, long, default_value = "@")]
    28→    pub revision: String,
    29→
    30→    /// Number of commits to scan
    31→    #[arg(short = 'n', long = "commits", value_name = "COMMITS")]
    32→    pub limit: Option<usize>,
    33→
    34→    /// Maximum number of commits to actually fix/change [default: all]
    35→    #[arg(short = 'm', long = "max-fixes")]
    36→    pub max_fixes: Option<usize>,
    37→
    38→    /// Path to jj repo
    39→    #[arg(short = 'R', long, default_value = ".")]
    40→    pub repo: PathBuf,
    41→
    42→    /// Path to the other repo (e.g. .claude or .)
    43→    #[arg(long = "other-repo")]
    44→    pub other_repo: PathBuf,
    45→
    46→    /// Expected changeID length
    47→    #[arg(long = "id-len", default_value_t = DEFAULT_ID_LEN)]
    48→    pub id_len: usize,
    49→
    50→    /// New title to replace the first line (optional)
    51→    #[arg(long)]
    52→    pub title: Option<String>,
    53→
    54→    /// Fallback ochid value for IDs not found in other repo (e.g. /.claude/lost)
    55→    #[arg(long)]
    56→    pub fallback: Option<String>,
    57→
    58→    /// Actually write changes (default is dry-run)
    59→    #[arg(long = "no-dry-run")]
    60→    pub no_dry_run: bool,
    61→
    62→    /// Add missing ochid trailers by matching title and timestamp
    63→    #[arg(long = "add-missing")]
    64→    pub add_missing: bool,
    65→}
    66→
    67→/// Derive the ochid prefix from the other repo's `.vc-config.toml`.
    68→///
    69→/// The `workspace.path` value is the repo's path relative to the workspace
    70→/// root, which is exactly the ochid prefix (with a trailing `/` when the
    71→/// path isn't just `/`).
    72→fn ochid_prefix_from_config(
    73→    config: &std::collections::HashMap<String, String>,
    74→) -> Result<String, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    75→    let ws_path = toml_simple::toml_get(config, "workspace.path")
    76→        .ok_or("missing workspace.path in .vc-config.toml")?;
    77→    let trimmed = ws_path.trim_end_matches('/');
    78→    if trimmed.is_empty() {
    79→        Ok("/".to_string())
    80→    } else {
    81→        Ok(format!("{trimmed}/"))
    82→    }
    83→}
    84→
    85→/// Problems found with an ochid trailer.
    86→#[derive(Debug)]
    87→struct OchidIssues {
    88→    wrong_prefix: bool,
    89→    wrong_length: bool,
    90→    not_found: bool,
    91→}
    92→
    93→impl OchidIssues {
    94→    fn any(&self) -> bool {
    95→        self.wrong_prefix || self.wrong_length || self.not_found
    96→    }
    97→
    98→    fn summary(&self) -> String {
    99→        let mut parts = Vec::new();
   100→        if self.wrong_prefix {
   101→            parts.push("wrong prefix");
   102→        }
   103→        if self.wrong_length {
   104→            parts.push("wrong ID length");
   105→        }
   106→        if self.not_found {
   107→            parts.push("ID not found in other repo");
   108→        }
   109→        parts.join(", ")
   110→    }
   111→}
   112→
   113→/// Validate an ochid trailer value against expected prefix, length, and other repo.
   114→fn validate_ochid(
   115→    value: &str,
   116→    other_prefix: &str,
   117→    id_len: usize,
   118→    other_workspace: &Workspace,
   119→    other_repo: &Arc<ReadonlyRepo>,
   120→) -> OchidIssues {
   121→    let wrong_prefix = !value.starts_with(other_prefix);
   122→
   123→    // Extract the bare ID
   124→    let bare_id = if let Some(pos) = value.rfind('/') {
   125→        &value[pos + 1..]
   126→    } else {
   127→        value
   128→    };
   129→
   130→    let wrong_length = bare_id.len() != id_len;
   131→
   132→    // Check if the ID resolves in the other repo
   133→    let not_found = common::resolve_revset(other_workspace, other_repo, bare_id)
   134→        .map(|ids| ids.is_empty())
   135→        .unwrap_or(true);
   136→
   137→    OchidIssues {
   138→        wrong_prefix,
   139→        wrong_length,
   140→        not_found,
   141→    }
   142→}
   143→
   144→/// Fix the ochid trailer in a commit description.
   145→///
   146→/// If `resolved_id` is provided, it replaces the bare changeID entirely
   147→/// (used when the existing ID is too short to extend by truncation alone).
   148→fn fix_ochid_in_description(
   149→    desc: &str,
   150→    other_prefix: &str,
   151→    id_len: usize,
   152→    new_title: Option<&str>,
   153→    resolved_id: Option<&str>,
   154→) -> String {
   155→    let mut lines: Vec<String> = desc.lines().map(|l| l.to_string()).collect();
   156→
   157→    // Fix title if requested
   158→    if let Some(title) = new_title
   159→        && !lines.is_empty()
   160→    {
   161→        lines[0] = title.to_string();
   162→    }
   163→
   164→    // Fix ochid trailer
   165→    for line in &mut lines {
   166→        let trimmed = line.trim();
   167→        if let Some(value) = trimmed.strip_prefix("ochid:") {
   168→            let value = value.trim();
   169→            let id = if let Some(rid) = resolved_id {
   170→                &rid[..rid.len().min(id_len)]
   171→            } else {
   172→                // Extract the bare changeID (strip any existing path prefix)
   173→                let bare_id = if let Some(pos) = value.rfind('/') {
   174→                    &value[pos + 1..]
   175→                } else {
   176→                    value
   177→                };
   178→                // Normalize to id_len chars
   179→                &bare_id[..bare_id.len().min(id_len)]
   180→            };
   181→            *line = format!("ochid: {other_prefix}{id}");
   182→        }
   183→    }
   184→
   185→    // Preserve trailing newline if original had one
   186→    let mut result = lines.join("\n");
   187→    if desc.ends_with('\n') {
   188→        result.push('\n');
   189→    }
   190→    result
   191→}
   192→
   193→/// Maximum timestamp difference (in milliseconds) for title-based matching.
   194→const TIMESTAMP_TOLERANCE_MS: i64 = 60_000;
   195→
   196→/// Find a unique matching commit in the other repo by title and timestamp.
   197→///
   198→/// Returns the other commit's change ID (full hex) if exactly one commit
   199→/// in the other repo has the same first description line and a committer
   200→/// timestamp within `TIMESTAMP_TOLERANCE_MS` of the source commit.
   201→fn find_matching_commit(
   202→    commit: &jj_lib::commit::Commit,
   203→    other_workspace: &Workspace,
   204→    other_repo: &Arc<ReadonlyRepo>,
   205→) -> Result<Option<String>, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
   206→    let title = commit.description().lines().next().unwrap_or("");
   207→    if title.is_empty() {
   208→        return Ok(None);
   209→    }
   210→    let src_millis = commit.committer().timestamp.timestamp.0;
   211→
   212→    // Search all commits in the other repo
   213→    let all_ids = common::resolve_revset(other_workspace, other_repo, "all()")?;
   214→    let root_id = other_repo.store().root_commit_id().clone();
   215→
   216→    let mut matches = Vec::new();
   217→    for cid in &all_ids {
   218→        if *cid == root_id {
   219→            continue;
   220→        }
   221→        let other_commit = other_repo.store().get_commit(cid)?;
   222→        let other_title = other_commit.description().lines().next().unwrap_or("");
   223→        if other_title != title {
   224→            continue;
   225→        }
   226→        let other_millis = other_commit.committer().timestamp.timestamp.0;
   227→        if (src_millis - other_millis).abs() <= TIMESTAMP_TOLERANCE_MS {
   228→            let full_hex =
   229→                jj_lib::hex_util::encode_reverse_hex(other_commit.change_id().as_bytes());
   230→            matches.push(full_hex);
   231→        }
   232→    }
   233→
   234→    if matches.len() == 1 {
   235→        Ok(Some(matches.into_iter().next().unwrap()))
   236→    } else {
   237→        Ok(None)
   238→    }
   239→}
   240→
   241→/// Append an ochid trailer to a commit description.
   242→fn append_ochid_trailer(desc: &str, other_prefix: &str, change_id: &str, id_len: usize) -> String {
   243→    let short_id = &change_id[..change_id.len().min(id_len)];
   244→    let trailer = format!("ochid: {other_prefix}{short_id}");
   245→
   246→    let mut result = desc.trim_end().to_string();
   247→    // Add blank line before trailer if body exists, otherwise just newline
   248→    if result.lines().count() > 1 {
   249→        result.push('\n');
   250→    } else {
   251→        result.push_str("\n\n");
   252→    }
   253→    result.push_str(&trailer);
   254→    result.push('\n');
   255→    result
   256→}
   257→
   258→pub fn fix_ochid(args: &FixOchidArgs) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
   259→    let (workspace, repo) = common::load_repo(&args.repo)?;
   260→    let (other_workspace, other_repo) = common::load_repo(&args.other_repo)?;
   261→    let other_config = toml_simple::toml_load(&args.other_repo.join(VC_CONFIG_FILE))?;
   262→    let other_prefix = ochid_prefix_from_config(&other_config)?;
   263→
   264→    let spec = common::resolve_spec(
   265→        args.pos_rev.as_deref(),
   266→        args.pos_count,
   267→        &args.revision,
   268→        args.limit,
   269→        "@",
   270→    );
   271→
   272→    let (ids, _anchor_index) = common::collect_ids(
   273→        &workspace,
   274→        &repo,
   275→        &spec.rev,
   276→        spec.desc_count,
   277→        spec.anc_count,
   278→    )?;
   279→    if ids.is_empty() {
   280→        return Err(format!("no commits found for revision '{}'", args.revision).into());
   281→    }
   282→
   283→    let root_id = repo.store().root_commit_id().clone();
   284→    let mut fixed = 0;
   285→    let mut skipped = 0;
   286→    let mut errors = 0;
   287→
   288→    for commit_id in &ids {
   289→        if *commit_id == root_id {
   290→            continue;
   291→        }
   292→        let commit = repo.store().get_commit(commit_id)?;
   293→        let desc = commit.description();
   294→        let change_hex = jj_lib::hex_util::encode_reverse_hex(commit.change_id().as_bytes());
   295→        let change_short = &change_hex[..change_hex.len().min(12)];
   296→
   297→        // Extract current ochid value
   298→        let current_ochid = common::extract_ochid(&commit);
   299→
   300→        // Validate current ochid
   301→        let issues = if let Some(ref ochid_val) = current_ochid {
   302→            validate_ochid(
   303→                ochid_val,
   304→                &other_prefix,
   305→                args.id_len,
   306→                &other_workspace,
   307→                &other_repo,
   308→            )
   309→        } else if args.add_missing {
   310→            // Stop if we've hit the max-fixes limit
   311→            if let Some(max) = args.max_fixes
   312→                && fixed >= max
   313→            {
   314→                skipped += 1;
   315→                if !args.no_dry_run {
   316→                    println!("skip {change_short}  (max-fixes reached)");
   317→                }
   318→                continue;
   319→            }
   320→            // No ochid trailer — try to infer from the other repo
   321→            if let Some(matched_id) = find_matching_commit(&commit, &other_workspace, &other_repo)?
   322→            {
   323→                let new_desc = append_ochid_trailer(desc, &other_prefix, &matched_id, args.id_len);
   324→                let first_line = desc.lines().next().unwrap_or("");
   325→                let short_matched = &matched_id[..matched_id.len().min(args.id_len)];
   326→                if !args.no_dry_run {
   327→                    println!("add  {change_short}  {first_line}  [missing]");
   328→                    println!("     -> ochid: {other_prefix}{short_matched}");
   329→                } else {
   330→                    let status = Command::new("jj")
   331→                        .arg("describe")
   332→                        .arg("-m")
   333→                        .arg(&new_desc)
   334→                        .arg("-r")
   335→                        .arg(commit_id.hex())
   336→                        .arg("-R")
   337→                        .arg(&args.repo)
   338→                        .arg("--ignore-immutable")
   339→                        .stdout(std::process::Stdio::null())
   340→                        .stderr(std::process::Stdio::inherit())
   341→                        .status()?;
   342→                    if !status.success() {
   343→                        return Err(format!(
   344→                            "jj describe failed for {} (exit {})",
   345→                            change_short,
   346→                            status.code().unwrap_or(-1)
   347→                        )
   348→                        .into());
   349→                    }
   350→                    println!("added {change_short}  {first_line}");
   351→                }
   352→                fixed += 1;
   353→                continue;
   354→            } else {
   355→                skipped += 1;
   356→                if !args.no_dry_run {
   357→                    println!("skip {change_short}  (no ochid, no match in other repo)");
   358→                }
   359→                continue;
   360→            }
   361→        } else {
   362→            // No ochid trailer — nothing to fix
   363→            skipped += 1;
   364→            if !args.no_dry_run {
   365→                println!("skip {change_short}  (no ochid trailer)");
   366→            }
   367→            continue;
   368→        };
   369→
   370→        if !issues.any() {
   371→            skipped += 1;
   372→            if !args.no_dry_run {
   373→                println!("ok   {change_short}  (valid)");
   374→            }
   375→            continue;
   376→        }
   377→
   378→        // Stop if we've hit the max-fixes limit
   379→        if let Some(max) = args.max_fixes
   380→            && fixed >= max
   381→        {
   382→            skipped += 1;
   383→            if !args.no_dry_run {
   384→                println!("skip {change_short}  (max-fixes reached)");
   385→            }
   386→            continue;
   387→        }
   388→
   389→        // Resolve the full change ID from the other repo when length is wrong
   390→        let resolved_id = if issues.wrong_length {
   391→            let ochid_val = current_ochid.as_deref().unwrap_or("");
   392→            let bare_id = if let Some(pos) = ochid_val.rfind('/') {
   393→                &ochid_val[pos + 1..]
   394→            } else {
   395→                ochid_val
   396→            };
   397→            if let Ok(commit_ids) = common::resolve_revset(&other_workspace, &other_repo, bare_id)
   398→                && let Some(cid) = commit_ids.first()
   399→            {
   400→                let other_commit = other_repo.store().get_commit(cid)?;
   401→                let full_hex =
   402→                    jj_lib::hex_util::encode_reverse_hex(other_commit.change_id().as_bytes());
   403→                Some(full_hex)
   404→            } else {
   405→                None
   406→            }
   407→        } else {
   408→            None
   409→        };
   410→
   411→        // Build the fixed description
   412→        let mut new_desc = fix_ochid_in_description(
   413→            desc,
   414→            &other_prefix,
   415→            args.id_len,
   416→            args.title.as_deref(),
   417→            resolved_id.as_deref(),
   418→        );
   419→
   420→        // Re-extract the fixed ochid value for post-validation
   421→        let mut fixed_ochid = {
   422→            let mut val = None;
   423→            for line in new_desc.lines() {
   424→                let trimmed = line.trim();
   425→                if let Some(v) = trimmed.strip_prefix("ochid:") {
   426→                    val = Some(v.trim().to_string());
   427→                }
   428→            }
   429→            val
   430→        };
   431→
   432→        let post_issues = if let Some(ref v) = fixed_ochid {
   433→            validate_ochid(v, &other_prefix, args.id_len, &other_workspace, &other_repo)
   434→        } else {
   435→            OchidIssues {
   436→                wrong_prefix: false,
   437→                wrong_length: false,
   438→                not_found: true,
   439→            }
   440→        };
   441→
   442→        if post_issues.not_found {
   443→            if let Some(ref fallback) = args.fallback {
   444→                // Replace the ochid line with the fallback value
   445→                let mut fb_lines: Vec<String> = new_desc.lines().map(|l| l.to_string()).collect();
   446→                for line in &mut fb_lines {
   447→                    if line.trim().starts_with("ochid:") {
   448→                        *line = format!("ochid: {fallback}");
   449→                    }
   450→                }
   451→                let fb_desc = if new_desc.ends_with('\n') {
   452→                    format!("{}\n", fb_lines.join("\n"))
   453→                } else {
   454→                    fb_lines.join("\n")
   455→                };
   456→                new_desc = fb_desc;
   457→                fixed_ochid = Some(fallback.clone());
   458→            } else {
   459→                errors += 1;
   460→                eprintln!(
   461→                    "err  {change_short}  ID not found in other repo (ochid: {})",
   462→                    fixed_ochid.as_deref().unwrap_or("?")
   463→                );
   464→                continue;
   465→            }
   466→        }
   467→
   468→        if !args.no_dry_run {
   469→            let first_line = new_desc.lines().next().unwrap_or("");
   470→            println!("fix  {change_short}  {first_line}  [{}]", issues.summary());
   471→            if let Some(ref v) = fixed_ochid {
   472→                println!("     -> ochid: {v}");
   473→            }
   474→        } else {
   475→            let status = Command::new("jj")
   476→                .arg("describe")
   477→                .arg("-m")
   478→                .arg(&new_desc)
   479→                .arg("-r")
   480→                .arg(commit_id.hex())
   481→                .arg("-R")
   482→                .arg(&args.repo)
   483→                .arg("--ignore-immutable")
   484→                .stdout(std::process::Stdio::null())
   485→                .stderr(std::process::Stdio::inherit())
   486→                .status()?;
   487→            if !status.success() {
   488→                return Err(format!(
   489→                    "jj describe failed for {} (exit {})",
   490→                    change_short,
   491→                    status.code().unwrap_or(-1)
   492→                )
   493→                .into());
   494→            }
   495→            let first_line = new_desc.lines().next().unwrap_or("");
   496→            println!("fixed {change_short}  {first_line}");
   497→        }
   498→        fixed += 1;
   499→    }
   500→
   501→    println!(
   502→        "\n{fixed} fixed, {skipped} skipped, {errors} errors (of {} total)",
   503→        ids.len()
   504→    );
   505→    if errors > 0 {
   506→        Err(format!("{errors} commit(s) could not be fixed").into())
   507→    } else {
   508→        Ok(())
   509→    }
   510→}
   511→
   512→#[cfg(test)]
   513→mod tests {
   514→    use super::*;
   515→
   516→    fn make_config(ws_path: &str) -> std::collections::HashMap<String, String> {
   517→        let mut map = std::collections::HashMap::new();
   518→        map.insert("workspace.path".to_string(), ws_path.to_string());
   519→        map
   520→    }
   521→
   522→    #[test]
   523→    fn prefix_from_root() {
   524→        let config = make_config("/");
   525→        assert_eq!(ochid_prefix_from_config(&config).unwrap(), "/");
   526→    }
   527→
   528→    #[test]
   529→    fn prefix_from_claude() {
   530→        let config = make_config("/.claude");
   531→        assert_eq!(ochid_prefix_from_config(&config).unwrap(), "/.claude/");
   532→    }
   533→
   534→    #[test]
   535→    fn prefix_from_nested_path() {
   536→        let config = make_config("/some/path");
   537→        assert_eq!(ochid_prefix_from_config(&config).unwrap(), "/some/path/");
   538→    }
   539→
   540→    #[test]
   541→    fn prefix_missing_config_key() {
   542→        let config = std::collections::HashMap::new();
   543→        assert!(ochid_prefix_from_config(&config).is_err());
   544→    }
   545→
   546→    #[test]
   547→    fn fix_bare_id() {
   548→        let desc = "Some title\n\nBody text.\n\nochid: tzupykyyvnrp\n";
   549→        let result = fix_ochid_in_description(desc, "/.claude/", DEFAULT_ID_LEN, None, None);
   550→        assert!(result.contains("ochid: /.claude/tzupykyyvnrp"));
   551→        assert!(result.starts_with("Some title\n"));
   552→    }
   553→
   554→    #[test]
   555→    fn fix_wrong_prefix() {
   556→        let desc = "Title\n\nochid: /wrong/tzupykyyvnrp\n";
   557→        let result = fix_ochid_in_description(desc, "/.claude/", DEFAULT_ID_LEN, None, None);
   558→        assert!(result.contains("ochid: /.claude/tzupykyyvnrp"));
   559→    }
   560→
   561→    #[test]
   562→    fn already_correct() {
   563→        let desc = "Title\n\nochid: /.claude/tzupykyyvnrp\n";
   564→        let result = fix_ochid_in_description(desc, "/.claude/", DEFAULT_ID_LEN, None, None);
   565→        assert_eq!(result, desc);
   566→    }
   567→
   568→    #[test]
   569→    fn truncate_long_id() {
   570→        let desc = "Title\n\nochid: abcdefghijklmnop\n";
   571→        let result = fix_ochid_in_description(desc, "/", DEFAULT_ID_LEN, None, None);
   572→        assert!(result.contains("ochid: /abcdefghijkl"));
   573→        assert!(!result.contains("mnop"));
   574→    }
   575→
   576→    #[test]
   577→    fn fix_title_and_ochid() {
   578→        let desc = "Old title\n\nochid: bare12345678\n";
   579→        let result =
   580→            fix_ochid_in_description(desc, "/.claude/", DEFAULT_ID_LEN, Some("New title"), None);
   581→        assert!(result.starts_with("New title\n"));
   582→        assert!(result.contains("ochid: /.claude/bare12345678"));
   583→    }
   584→
   585→    #[test]
   586→    fn no_ochid_no_change() {
   587→        let desc = "Title\n\nNo trailer here.\n";
   588→        let result = fix_ochid_in_description(desc, "/.claude/", DEFAULT_ID_LEN, None, None);
   589→        assert_eq!(result, desc);
   590→    }
   591→
   592→    #[test]

OchidIssues, DEFAULT_ID_LEN, VC_CONFIG_FILE,\n};\nuse crate::toml_simple;\n\n#[derive(Args, Debug)]\npub struct FixOchidArgs {\n    /// Revision (with optional .. notation)\n    #[arg(value_name = \"add-missing\")]\n    pub add_missing: bool,\n}\n\npub fn fix_ochid(args: &FixOchidArgs) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let (workspace, repo) = common::load_repo(&args.repo)?;\n    let (other_workspace, other_repo) = common::load_repo(&args.other_repo)?;\n    let other_config = toml_simple::toml_load(&args.other_repo.join(VC_CONFIG_FILE))?;\n    let other_prefix = ochid_prefix_from_config(&other_config)?;\n\n    let spec = common::resolve_spec(\n        args.pos_rev.as_deref(),\n        args.pos_count,\n        &args.revision,\n        args.limit,\n        \"@\",\n    );\n\n    let (ids, _anchor_index) = common::collect_ids(\n        &workspace,\n        &repo,\n        &spec.rev,\n        spec.desc_count,\n        spec.anc_count,\n    )?;\n    if ids.is_empty() {\n        return Err(format!(\"no commits found for revision '{}'\", args.revision).into());\n    }\n\n    let root_id = repo.store().root_commit_id().clone();\n    let mut fixed = 0;\n    let mut skipped = 0;\n    let mut errors = 0;\n\n    for commit_id in &ids {\n        if *commit_id == root_id {\n            continue;\n        }\n        let commit = repo.store().get_commit(commit_id)?;\n        let desc = commit.description();\n        let change_hex = jj_lib::hex_util::encode_reverse_hex(commit.change_id().as_bytes());\n        let change_short = &change_hex[..change_hex.len().min(12)];\n\n        // Extract current ochid value\n        let current_ochid = common::extract_ochid(&commit);\n\n        // Validate current ochid\n        let issues = if let Some(ref ochid_val) = current_ochid {\n            validate_ochid(\n                ochid_val,\n                &other_prefix,\n                args.id_len,\n                &other_workspace,\n                &other_repo,\n            )\n        } else if args.add_missing {\n            // Stop if we've hit the max-fixes limit\n            if let Some(max) = args.max_fixes\n                && fixed >= max\n            {\n                skipped += 1;\n                if !args.no_dry_run {\n                    println!(\"skip {change_short}  (max-fixes reached)\");\n                }\n                continue;\n            }\n            // No ochid trailer — try to infer from the other repo\n            if let Some(matched_id) = first_line = desc.lines().next().unwrap_or(\"\");\n                let short_matched = &matched_id[..matched_id.len().min(args.id_len)];\n                if !args.no_dry_run {\n                    println!(\"add  {change_short}  {first_line}  [missing]\");\n                    println!(\"     -> ochid: {other_prefix}{short_matched}\");\n                } else {\n                    let status = Command::new(\"jj\")\n                        .arg(\"describe\")\n                        .arg(\"-m\")\n                        .arg(&new_desc)\n                        .arg(\"-r\")\n 1;\n                continue;\n            } else {\n                skipped += 1;\n                if !args.no_dry_run {\n                    println!(\"skip {change_short}  (no ochid, no match in other repo)\");\n                }\n                continue;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // No ochid trailer — nothing to fix\n            skipped += 1;\n            if !args.no_dry_run {\n                println!(\"skip {change_short}  (no ochid trailer)\");\n            }\n            continue;\n        };\n\n        if !issues.any() {\n            skipped += 1;\n            if !args.no_dry_run {\n                println!(\"ok   {change_short}  (valid)\");\n            }\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // Stop if we've hit the max-fixes limit\n        if let Some(max) = args.max_fixes\n            && fixed >= max\n        {\n            skipped += 1;\n            if !args.no_dry_run {\n                println!(\"skip {change_short}  (max-fixes reached)\");\n            }\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // Resolve the full change ID from the other repo when length is wrong\n        let resolved_id = if issues.wrong_length {\n            let ochid_val = current_ochid.as_deref().unwrap_or(\"\");\n            let bare_id = extract_bare_id(ochid_val);\n            resolve_full_change_id(bare_id, &other_workspace, &other_repo)?\n        } else {\n            None\n        };\n\n        // Build the fixed description\n        let mut new_desc = fix_ochid_in_description(\n            desc,\n            &other_prefix,\n            args.id_len,\n            args.title.as_deref(),\n            resolved_id.as_deref(),\n        );\n\n        // Re-extract the fixed ochid value for post-validation\n        let mut fixed_ochid = extract_ochid_from_desc(&new_desc);\n\n        let post_issues = if let Some(ref v) = fixed_ochid {\n            validate_ochid(v, &other_prefix, args.id_len, &other_workspace, &other_repo)\n        } else {\n            OchidIssues {\n                wrong_prefix: false,\n                wrong_length: false,\n                not_found: true,\n            }\n        };\n\n        if post_issues.not_found {\n            if let Some(ref fallback) = args.fallback {\n                // Replace the ochid line with the fallback value\n                let mut fb_lines: Vec<String> = new_desc.lines().map(|l| l.to_string()).collect();\n                for line in &mut fb_lines {\n                    if line.trim().starts_with(\"ochid:\") {\n                        *line = format!(\"ochid: {fallback}\");\n                    }\n                }\n                let fb_desc = if new_desc.ends_with('\\n') {\n                    format!(\"{}\\n\", fb_lines.join(\"\\n\"))\n                } else {\n                    fb_lines.join(\"\\n\")\n                };\n                new_desc = fb_desc;\n                fixed_ochid = Some(fallback.clone());\n            } else {\n                errors += 1;\n                eprintln!(\n                    \"err  {change_short}  ID not found in other repo (ochid: {})\",\n new_desc.lines().next().unwrap_or(\"\");\n            println!(\"fix  {change_short}  {first_line}  [{}]\", issues.summary());\n            if let Some(ref v) = fixed_ochid {\n                println!(\"     -> ochid: {v}\");\n            }\n        } else {\n            let status = Command::new(\"jj\")\n                .arg(\"describe\")\n                .arg(\"-m\")\n                .arg(&new_desc)\n                .arg(\"-r\")\n                .arg(commit_id.hex())\n                .arg(\"-R\")\n                .arg(&args.repo)\n                .arg(\"--ignore-immutable\")\n .into());\n            }\n            let first_line = new_desc.lines().next().unwrap_or(\"\");\n            println!(\"fixed {change_short}  {first_line}\");\n        }\n        fixed += 1;\n    }\n\n    println!(\n        \"\\n{fixed} fixed, {skipped} skipped, {errors} errors (of {} total)\",\n        ids.len()\n    );\n    if errors > 0 {\n        Err(format!(\"{errors} commit(s) could not    138→}
   139→
   140→/// Resolve a short change ID to its full hex via the other repo.
   141→pub fn resolve_full_change_id(
   142→    bare_id: &str,
   143→    other_workspace: &Workspace,
   144→    other_repo: &Arc<ReadonlyRepo>,
   145→) -> Result<Option<String>, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
   146→    if let Ok(commit_ids) = common::resolve_revset(other_workspace, other_repo, bare_id)
   147→        && let Some(cid) = commit_ids.first()
   148→    {
   149→        let other_commit = other_repo.store().get_commit(cid)?;
   150→        let full_hex = jj_lib::hex_util::encode_reverse_hex(other_commit.change_id().as_bytes());
   151→        Ok(Some(full_hex))
   152→    } else {
   153→        Ok(None)
   154→    }
   155→}
   156→
   157→/// Extract the bare change ID from an ochid value (strip path prefix).
   158→pub fn extract_bare_id(ochid_value: &str) -> &str {
   159→    if let Some(pos) = ochid_value.rfind('/') {
   160→        &ochid_value[pos + 1..]
   161→    } else {
   162→        ochid_value
   163→    }
   164→}
   165→
   166→/// Fix the ochid trailer in a commit description.
   167→///
   168→/// If `resolved_id` is provided, it replaces the bare changeID entirely
   169→/// (used when the existing ID is too short to extend by truncation alone).
   170→pub fn fix_ochid_in_description(
   171→    desc: &str,
   172→    other_prefix: &str,
   173→    id_len: usize,
   174→    new_title: Option<&str>,
   175→    resolved_id: Option<&str>,
   176→) -> String {
   177→    let mut lines: Vec<String> = desc.lines().map(|l| l.to_string()).collect();
   178→
   179→    // Fix title if requested
   180→    if let Some(title) = new_title
   181→        && !lines.is_empty()
   182→    {
   183→        lines[0] = title.to_string();
   184→    }
   185→
   186→    // Fix ochid trailer
   187→    for line in &mut lines {
   188→        let trimmed = line.trim();
   189→        if let Some(value) = trimmed.strip_prefix("ochid:") {
   190→            let value = value.trim();
   191→            let id = if let Some(rid) = resolved_id {
   192→                &rid[..rid.len().min(id_len)]
   193→            } else {
   194→                let bare_id = extract_bare_id(value);
   195→                &bare_id[..bare_id.len().min(id_len)]
   196→            };
   197→            *line = format!("ochid: {other_prefix}{id}");
   198→        }
   199→    }
   200→
   201→    // Preserve trailing newline if original had one
   202→    let mut result = lines.join("\n");
   203→    if desc.ends_with('\n') {
   204→        result.push('\n');
   205→    }
   206→    result
   207→}
   208→
   209→/// Append an ochid trailer to a commit description.
   210→pub fn append_ochid_trailer(
   211→    desc: &str,
   212→    other_prefix: &str,
   213→    change_id: &str,
   214→    id_len: usize,
   215→) -> String {
   216→    let short_id = &change_id[..change_id.len().min(id_len)];
   217→    let trailer = format!("ochid: {other_prefix}{short_id}");
   218→
   219→    let mut result = desc.trim_end().to_string();
   220→    // Add blank line before trailer if body exists, otherwise just newline
   221→    if result.lines().count() > 1 {
   222→        result.push('\n');
   223→    } else {
   224→        result.push_str("\n\n");
   225→    }
   226→    result.push_str(&trailer);
   227→    result.push('\n');
   228→    result
   229→}
   230→
   231→/// Extract the ochid value from a description string (without needing a Commit).
   232→pub fn extract_ochid_from_desc(desc: &str) -> Option<String> {
   233→    for line in desc.lines() {
   234→        let trimmed = line.trim();
   235→        if let Some(v) = trimmed.strip_prefix("ochid:") {
   236→            return Some(v.trim().to_string());
   237→        }

2. Add the status legend to the help text
3. `--other-repo` as a positional argument — hmm, it's required so it could be, but it conflicts with the existing `REVISION` and `COMMITS` positionals. Makes sense — it's required anyway, so a positional is cleaner. The usage would become:

```
vc-x1 validate-desc <OTHER_REPO> [REVISION] [COMMITS] [OPTIONS]
```

But ordering matters for positionals. `OTHER_REPO` first, then optional `REVISION` and `COMMITS`? That changes the order from fix-ochid where `REVISION` is first. Or should it match and go `REVISION COMMITS OTHER_REPO`?

fix-ochid stays unchanged fix-desc will/should have identical positional params as validate?

Right. So for validate-desc and fix-desc:

```
vc-x1 validate-desc <OTHER_REPO> [REVISION] [COMMITS]

    48→/// Status of a single commit's ochid validation.
    49→enum CommitStatus {
    50→    Ok,
    51→    NeedsFixed(String),       // summary of issues

    53→    MissingWithMatch(String), // no ochid, but match found in other repo
    54→    Skip(String),             // skipped with reason
    55→}
    56→
    57→pub fn validate_desc(args: &ValidateDescArgs) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    58→    let (workspace, repo) = common::load_repo(&args.repo)?;
    59→    let (other_workspace, other_repo) = common::load_repo(&args.other_repo)?;
    60→    let other_config = toml_simple::toml_load(&args.other_repo.join(VC_CONFIG_FILE))?;
    61→    let other_prefix = ochid_prefix_from_config(&other_config)?;
    62→
    63→    let spec = common::resolve_spec(
    64→        args.pos_rev.as_deref(),
    65→        args.pos_count,
    66→        &args.revision,
    67→        args.limit,
    68→        "@",
    69→    );
    70→
    71→    let (ids, _anchor_index) = common::collect_ids(
    72→        &workspace,
    73→        &repo,
    74→        &spec.rev,
    75→        spec.desc_count,
    76→        spec.anc_count,
    77→    )?;
    78→    if ids.is_empty() {
    79→        return Err(format!("no commits found for revision '{}'", args.revision).into());
    80→    }
    81→
    82→    let root_id = repo.store().root_commit_id().clone();
    83→    let mut valid = 0;
    84→    let mut issues_count = 0;
    85→    let mut missing = 0;
    86→    let mut skipped = 0;
    87→
    88→    for commit_id in &ids {
    89→        if *commit_id == root_id {
    90→            continue;
    91→        }
    92→        let commit = repo.store().get_commit(commit_id)?;
    93→        let desc = commit.description();
    94→        let change_hex = jj_lib::hex_util::encode_reverse_hex(commit.change_id().as_bytes());
    95→        let change_short = &change_hex[..change_hex.len().min(12)];
    96→        let first_line = desc.lines().next().unwrap_or("");
    97→
    98→        let current_ochid = common::extract_ochid(&commit);
    99→
   100→        let status = if let Some(ref ochid_val) = current_ochid {
   101→            let issues = validate_ochid(
   102→                ochid_val,
   103→                &other_prefix,
   104→                args.id_len,
   105→                &other_workspace,
   106→                &other_repo,
   107→            );
   108→            if issues.any() {
   109→                CommitStatus::NeedsFixed(issues.summary())
   110→            } else {
   111→                CommitStatus::Ok
   112→            }
   113→        } else if args.check_missing {
   114→            if let Some(matched_id) = find_matching_commit(&commit, &other_workspace, &other_repo)?
   115→            {
   116→                let short_id = &matched_id[..matched_id.len().min(args.id_len)];
   117→                CommitStatus::MissingWithMatch(format!("{other_prefix}{short_id}"))
   118→            } else {
   119→                CommitStatus::Missing
   120→            }
   121→        } else {
   122→            CommitStatus::Skip("no ochid trailer".to_string())
   123→        };
   124→
   125→        match status {
   126→            CommitStatus::Ok => {
   127→                valid += 1;
   128→                println!("ok   {change_short}  {first_line}");
   129→            }
   130→            CommitStatus::NeedsFixed(summary) => {
   131→                issues_count += 1;
   132→                println!("err  {change_short}  {first_line}  [{summary}]");
   133→            }
   134→            CommitStatus::MissingWithMatch(ochid) => {
   135→                missing += 1;
   136→                println!("miss {change_short}  {first_line}  [match: {ochid}]");
   137→            }
   138→            CommitStatus::Missing => {
   139→                missing += 1;
   140→                println!("miss {change_short}  {first_line}  [no match in other repo]");
   141→            }
   142→            CommitStatus::Skip(reason) => {
   143→                skipped += 1;
   144→                println!("skip {change_short}  ({reason})");
   145→            }
   146→        }
   147→    }
   148→
   149→    println!(
   150→        "\n{valid} valid, {issues_count} issues, {missing} missing, {skipped} skipped (of {} total)",
   151→        ids.len()
   152→    );
   153→    if issues_count > 0 {
   154→        Err(format!("{issues_count} commit(s) have issues").into())
   155→    } else {
   156→        Ok(())
   157→    }
   158→}
   159→

ochid_prefix_from_config, validate_ochid, DEFAULT_ID_LEN, VC_CONFIG_FILE,\n};\nuse crate::toml_simple;\n\n/// Validate ochid trailers in commit descriptions.\n///\n/// Status labels:\n///   ok   — ochid trailer is valid\n///   err  — ochid has issues (wrong prefix, wrong length, ID not found)\n///   miss — no ochid trailer (with or without a match in other repo)\n#[derive(Args, Debug)]\npub struct ValidateDescArgs {\n    /// Path to the other repo (e.g. .claude or .)\n    pub other_repo: PathBuf,\n\n    /// Revision (with optional .. notation)\n    #[arg(value_name = \"REVISION\")]\n    pub pos_rev: Option<String>,\n\n    /// Number of commits (per dotted side)\n    #[arg(value_name = \"COMMITS\")]\n    pub pos_count: Option<usize>,\n\n    /// Revision(s) to scan\n    #[arg(short, long, default_value = \"@\")]\n    pub revision: String,\n\n    /// Number of commits to scan\n    #[arg(short = 'n', long = \"commits\", value_name = \"COMMITS\")]\n    pub limit: Option<usize>,\n\n    /// Path to jj repo\n    #[arg(short = 'R', long, default_value = \".\")]\n    pub repo: PathBuf,\n\n    /// Expected changeID length\n    #[arg(long = \"id-len\", default_value_t = DEFAULT_ID_LEN)]\n    pub id_len: usize,\n}\n\n/// Status of a single commit's ochid validation.\nenum CommitStatus {\n    Ok,\n    NeedsFixed(String),       // summary of issues\n    Missing,                  // no ochid trailer\n    MissingWithMatch(String), // no ochid, but match found in other repo\n}\n\npub fn validate_desc(args: &ValidateDescArgs) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let (workspace, repo) = common::load_repo(&args.repo)?;\n    let (other_workspace, other_repo) = common::load_repo(&args.other_repo)?;\n    let other_config = toml_simple::toml_load(&args.other_repo.join(VC_CONFIG_FILE))?;\n    let other_prefix = ochid_prefix_from_config(&other_config)?;\n\n    let spec = common::resolve_spec(\n        args.pos_rev.as_deref(),\n        args.pos_count,\n        &args.revision,\n        args.limit,\n        \"@\",\n    );\n\n    let (ids, _anchor_index) = common::collect_ids(\n        &workspace,\n        &repo,\n        &spec.rev,\n        spec.desc_count,\n        spec.anc_count,\n    )?;\n    if ids.is_empty() {\n        return Err(format!(\"no commits found for revision '{}'\", args.revision).into());\n    }\n\n    let root_id = repo.store().root_commit_id().clone();\n    let mut valid = 0;\n    let mut issues_count = 0;\n    let mut missing = 0;\n\n    for commit_id in &ids {\n        if *commit_id == root_id {\n            continue;\n        }\n        let commit = repo.store().get_commit(commit_id)?;\n        let desc = commit.description();\n        let change_hex = jj_lib::hex_util::encode_reverse_hex(commit.change_id().as_bytes());\n        let change_short = &change_hex[..change_hex.len().min(12)];\n        let first_line = desc.lines().next().unwrap_or(\"\");\n\n        let current_ochid = common::extract_ochid(&commit);\n\n        let status = if let Some(ref ochid_val) = current_ochid {\n            let issues = validate_ochid(\n                ochid_val,\n                &other_prefix,\n                args.id_len,\n                &other_workspace,\n                &other_repo,\n            );\n            if issues.any() {\n                CommitStatus::NeedsFixed(issues.summary())\n            } else {\n                CommitStatus::Ok\n            }\n        } else if let Some(matched_id) =\n            find_matching_commit(&commit, &other_workspace, &other_repo)?\n        {\n            let short_id = &matched_id[..matched_id.len().min(args.id_len)];\n            CommitStatus::MissingWithMatch(format!(\"{other_prefix}{short_id}\"))\n        } else {\n            CommitStatus::Missing\n        };\n\n        match status {\n            CommitStatus::Ok => {\n                valid += 1;\n                println!(\"ok   {change_short}  {first_line}\");\n            }\n            CommitStatus::NeedsFixed(summary) => {\n                issues_count += 1;\n                println!(\"err  {change_short}  {first_line}  [{summary}]\");\n            }\n            CommitStatus::MissingWithMatch(ochid) => {\n                missing += 1;\n                println!(\"miss {change_short}  {first_line}  [match: {ochid}]\");\n            }\n            CommitStatus::Missing => {\n                missing += 1;\n                println!(\"miss {change_short}  {first_line}  [no match]\");\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    println!(\n        \"\\n{valid} valid, {issues_count} issues, {missing} missing (of {} total)\",\n        ids.len()\n    );\n    if issues_count > 0 {\n        Err(format!(\"{issues_count} commit(s) have issues\").into())\n    } else {\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

Better. Though now I'm wondering — should `[no match]` still show for a commit with no description? There's nothing to match on. But that's the correct behavior — it tried and found no match, which is accurate.

> DEVELOPER

I think the question is "no match" of what "no matching title in other-repo" is more precise. The thing is if your

> AGENT

If you're looking at `miss` with `(no description set)`, "no match" is confusing because there's nothing *to* match — the commit has no title. It's not that it searched and failed, it's that there was nothing to search for.

Maybe two distinct messages:
- `[no title]` — when the commit has no description (can't even attempt matching)
- `[no match in other repo]` — when it has a title but no matching commit was found

```
miss oxmzknnskzon  (no description set)

No bracket annotation needed when the reason is self-evident from the display title.

The data lines are `"miss oxmzknnskzon  ..."` — that's 4 chars + space + 12 chars + 2 spaces = column 19 for TITLE. The header `"STAT CHANGEID     TITLE"` — STAT(4) + space + CHANGEID(8) + 5 spaces + TITLE = column 19... let me count more carefully.

Data: `ok   lnynwxmzulxo  feat...` — `ok` + 3 spaces = 5, then 12 chars, then 2 spaces = position 19.
Header: `STAT CHANGEID     TITLE` — `STAT` + space = 5, `CHANGEID` = 8, then 5 spaces = 18, TITLE at position 19... wait, `CHANGEID` is 8 chars but the actual IDs are 12. So TITLE in the header is at position 18, but in the data at position 19.

  "content": "use std::path::PathBuf;\nuse std::process::Command;\n\nuse clap::Args;\nuse jj_lib::object_id::ObjectId;\nuse jj_lib::repo::Repo;\n\nuse crate::common;\nuse crate::desc_helpers::{\n    append_ochid_trailer, extract_bare_id, extract_ochid_from_desc, find_matching_commit,\n    fix_ochid_in_description, ochid_prefix_from_config, resolve_full_change_id, validate_ochid,\n    OchidIssues, DEFAULT_ID_LEN, VC_CONFIG_FILE,\n};\nuse crate::toml_simple;\n\n/// Fix commit descriptions against the other repo.\n#[derive(Args, Debug)]\npub struct FixDescArgs {\n    /// Path to the other repo (e.g. .claude or .)\n    pub other_repo: PathBuf,\n\n    /// Revision (with optional .. notation)\n    #[arg(value_name = \"REVISION\")]\n    pub pos_rev: Option<String>,\n\n    /// Number of commits (per dotted side)\n    #[arg(value_name = \"COMMITS\")]\n    pub pos_count: Option<usize>,\n\n    /// Revision(s) to scan\n    #[arg(short, long, default_value = \"@\")]\n    pub revision: String,\n\n    /// Number of commits to scan\n    #[arg(short = 'n', long = \"commits\", value_name = \"COMMITS\")]\n    pub limit: Option<usize>,\n\n    /// Maximum number of commits to actually fix/change [default: all]\n    #[arg(short = 'm', long = \"max-fixes\")]\n    pub max_fixes: Option<usize>,\n\n    /// Path to jj repo\n    #[arg(short = 'R', long, default_value = \".\")]\n    pub repo: PathBuf,\n\n    /// Expected changeID length\n    #[arg(long = \"id-len\", default_value_t = DEFAULT_ID_LEN)]\n    pub id_len: usize,\n\n    /// New title to replace the first line (optional)\n    #[arg(long)]\n    pub title: Option<String>,\n\n    /// Fallback ochid value for IDs not found in other repo (e.g. /.claude/lost)\n    #[arg(long)]\n    pub fallback: Option<String>,\n\n    /// Actually write changes (default is dry-run)\n    #[arg(long = \"no-dry-run\")]\n    pub no_dry_run: bool,\n\n    /// Add missing ochid trailers by matching title and timestamp\n    #[arg(long = \"add-missing\")]\n    pub add_missing: bool,\n}\n\npub fn fix_desc(args: &FixDescArgs) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let (workspace, repo) = common::load_repo(&args.repo)?;\n    let (other_workspace, other_repo) = common::load_repo(&args.other_repo)?;\n    let other_config = toml_simple::toml_load(&args.other_repo.join(VC_CONFIG_FILE))?;\n    let other_prefix = ochid_prefix_from_config(&other_config)?;\n\n    let spec = common::resolve_spec(\n        args.pos_rev.as_deref(),\n        args.pos_count,\n        &args.revision,\n        args.limit,\n        \"@\",\n    );\n\n    let (ids, _anchor_index) = common::collect_ids(\n        &workspace,\n        &repo,\n        &spec.rev,\n        spec.desc_count,\n        spec.anc_count,\n    )?;\n    if ids.is_empty() {\n        return Err(format!(\"no commits found for revision '{}'\", args.revision).into());\n    }\n\n    let root_id = repo.store().root_commit_id().clone();\n    let mut fixed = 0;\n    let mut skipped = 0;\n    let mut errors = 0;\n\n    for commit_id in &ids {\n        if *commit_id == root_id {\n            continue;\n        }\n        let commit = repo.store().get_commit(commit_id)?;\n        let desc = commit.description();\n        let change_hex = jj_lib::hex_util::encode_reverse_hex(commit.change_id().as_bytes());\n        let change_short = &change_hex[..change_hex.len().min(12)];\n        let first_line = desc.lines().next().unwrap_or(\"\");\n        let display_title = if first_line.is_empty() {\n            \"(no description set)\"\n        } else {\n            first_line\n        };\n\n        // Extract current ochid value\n        let current_ochid = common::extract_ochid(&commit);\n\n        // Validate current ochid\n        let issues = if let Some(ref ochid_val) = current_ochid {\n            validate_ochid(\n                ochid_val,\n                &other_prefix,\n                args.id_len,\n                &other_workspace,\n                &other_repo,\n            )\n        } else if args.add_missing {\n            // Stop if we've hit the max-fixes limit\n            if let Some(max) = args.max_fixes\n                && fixed >= max\n            {\n                skipped += 1;\n                if !args.no_dry_run {\n                    println!(\"skip {change_short}  {display_title}  (max-fixes reached)\");\n                }\n                continue;\n            }\n            // No ochid trailer — try to infer from the other repo\n            if first_line.is_empty() {\n                skipped += 1;\n                if !args.no_dry_run {\n                    println!(\"skip {change_short}  {display_title}\");\n                }\n                continue;\n            }\n            if let Some(matched_id) = find_matching_commit(&commit, &other_workspace, &other_repo)?\n            {\n                let new_desc = append_ochid_trailer(desc, &other_prefix, &matched_id, args.id_len);\n                let short_matched = &matched_id[..matched_id.len().min(args.id_len)];\n                if !args.no_dry_run {\n                    println!(\"add  {change_short}  {display_title}  [missing]\");\n                    println!(\"     -> ochid: {other_prefix}{short_matched}\");\n                } else {\n                    jj_describe(commit_id, &new_desc, &args.repo, change_short)?;\n                    println!(\"added {change_short}  {display_title}\");\n                }\n                fixed += 1;\n                continue;\n            } else {\n                skipped += 1;\n                if !args.no_dry_run {\n                    println!(\"skip {change_short}  {display_title}  (no match in other repo)\");\n                }\n                continue;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // No ochid trailer — nothing to fix\n            skipped += 1;\n            if !args.no_dry_run {\n                println!(\"skip {change_short}  {display_title}  (no ochid trailer)\");\n            }\n            continue;\n        };\n\n        if !issues.any() {\n            skipped += 1;\n            if !args.no_dry_run {\n                println!(\"ok   {change_short}  {display_title}\");\n            }\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // Stop if we've hit the max-fixes limit\n        if let Some(max) = args.max_fixes\n            && fixed >= max\n        {\n            skipped += 1;\n            if !args.no_dry_run {\n                println!(\"skip {change_short}  {display_title}  (max-fixes reached)\");\n            }\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // Resolve the full change ID from the other repo when length is wrong\n        let resolved_id = if issues.wrong_length {\n            let ochid_val = current_ochid.as_deref().unwrap_or(\"\");\n            let bare_id = extract_bare_id(ochid_val);\n            resolve_full_change_id(bare_id, &other_workspace, &other_repo)?\n        } else {\n            None\n        };\n\n        // Build the fixed description\n        let mut new_desc = fix_ochid_in_description(\n            desc,\n            &other_prefix,\n            args.id_len,\n            args.title.as_deref(),\n            resolved_id.as_deref(),\n        );\n\n        // Re-extract the fixed ochid value for post-validation\n        let mut fixed_ochid = extract_ochid_from_desc(&new_desc);\n\n        let post_issues = if let Some(ref v) = fixed_ochid {\n            validate_ochid(v, &other_prefix, args.id_len, &other_workspace, &other_repo)\n        } else {\n            OchidIssues {\n                wrong_prefix: false,\n                wrong_length: false,\n                not_found: true,\n            }\n        };\n\n        if post_issues.not_found {\n            if let Some(ref fallback) = args.fallback {\n                // Replace the ochid line with the fallback value\n                let mut fb_lines: Vec<String> = new_desc.lines().map(|l| l.to_string()).collect();\n                for line in &mut fb_lines {\n                    if line.trim().starts_with(\"ochid:\") {\n                        *line = format!(\"ochid: {fallback}\");\n                    }\n                }\n                let fb_desc = if new_desc.ends_with('\\n') {\n                    format!(\"{}\\n\", fb_lines.join(\"\\n\"))\n                } else {\n                    fb_lines.join(\"\\n\")\n                };\n                new_desc = fb_desc;\n                fixed_ochid = Some(fallback.clone());\n            } else {\n                errors += 1;\n                eprintln!(\n                    \"err  {change_short}  {display_title}  (ID not found, ochid: {})\",\n                    fixed_ochid.as_deref().unwrap_or(\"?\")\n                );\n                continue;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if !args.no_dry_run {\n            println!(\"fix  {change_short}  {display_title}  [{}]\", issues.summary());\n            if let Some(ref v) = fixed_ochid {\n                println!(\"     -> ochid: {v}\");\n            }\n        } else {\n            jj_describe(commit_id, &new_desc, &args.repo, change_short)?;\n            let fixed_title = new_desc.lines().next().unwrap_or(\"\");\n            println!(\"fixed {change_short}  {fixed_title}\");\n        }\n        fixed += 1;\n    }\n\n    println!(\n        \"\\n{fixed} fixed, {skipped} skipped, {errors} errors (of {} total)\",\n        ids.len()\n    );\n    if errors > 0 {\n        Err(format!(\"{errors} commit(s) could not be fixed\").into())\n    } else {\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n\n/// Run `jj describe` to rewrite a commit's description.\nfn jj_describe(\n    commit_id: &jj_lib::backend::CommitId,\n    new_desc: &str,\n    repo_path: &std::path::Path,\n    change_short: &str,\n) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let status = Command::new(\"jj\")\n        .arg(\"describe\")\n        .arg(\"-m\")\n        .arg(new_desc)\n        .arg(\"-r\")\n        .arg(commit_id.hex())\n        .arg(\"-R\")\n        .arg(repo_path)\n        .arg(\"--ignore-immutable\")\n        .stdout(std::process::Stdio::null())\n        .stderr(std::process::Stdio::inherit())\n        .status()?;\n    if !status.success() {\n        return Err(format!(\n            \"jj describe failed for {} (exit {})\",\n            change_short,\n            status.code().unwrap_or(-1)\n        )\n        .into());\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\n"
}
```

Fix commit descriptions against the other repo

Usage: vc-x1 fix-desc [OPTIONS] <OTHER_REPO> [REVISION] [COMMITS]

  [REVISION]    Revision (with optional .. notation)

Options:
  -r, --revision <REVISION>    Revision(s) to scan [default: @]
  -n, --commits <COMMITS>      Number of commits to scan
  -m, --max-fixes <MAX_FIXES>  Maximum number of commits to actually fix/change [default: all]
  -R, --repo <REPO>            Path to jj repo [default: .]
      --id-len <ID_LEN>        Expected changeID length [default: 12]
      --title <TITLE>          New title to replace the first line (optional)
      --fallback <FALLBACK>    Fallback ochid value for IDs not found in other repo (e.g. /.claude/lost)
      --no-dry-run             Actually write changes (default is dry-run)
      --add-missing            Add missing ochid trailers by matching title and timestamp
  -h, --help                   Print help (see more with '--help')

Always checks for potential matches by title and\ntimestamp in the other repo.\n\n```\n# Validate all ancestors of @\nvc-x1 validate-desc .claude @..\n\n# Validate specific range\nvc-x1 validate-desc .claude -r @.. -n 20\n```\n\nUse `--help` for the full status label legend.\n\n### fix-desc\n\nFix commit descriptions against the other repo. > DEVELOPER

So what types of issues cause "[no match in other repo]"

> AGENT

Looking at the code, `find_matching_commit` returns `None` (no match) when:

1. **Zero matches** — no commit in the other repo has the same title within 60 seconds
2. **Multiple matches** — more than one commit matches (ambiguous, so it won't guess)

So "no match in other repo" could mean:
- The counterpart commit was never made (e.g. early commits before the dual-repo workflow)
- The titles differ between repos (typo, reword after commit)
- The timestamps are more than 60 seconds apart
- Multiple commits share the same title within the time window

> AGENT

There it is — 08:17:02 vs 07:48:29. That's ~29 minutes apart, way beyond the 60-second tolerance. This is fixable because the titles match exactly — the timestamp tolerance is just too strict for this case.

So the improvement would be: when there's no match within 60s, check for title-only matches and report them with the time delta so the user can decide. Something like:

```
miss slmkmroqtqtp  Unify .. notation and CLI... [title match: ssokyszzwsxw, 29m apart]
```

That way the user knows it's fixable with `fix-desc` if we add a `--time-tolerance` flag or similar. Want me to implement this?

> DEVELOPER

sg, but can we make the "[reason]" more precise for all of the errors?

> AGENT

Yes. Let me think through all the cases:

1. **No title** — can't search → `(no title to match)`
2. **Title match, within tolerance** — `[match: /.claude/xyzxyzxyzxyz]` (already works)
3. **Title match, outside tolerance** — `[title match: ssokyszzwsxw, 29m apart]` (new)
4. **Multiple title matches within tolerance** — `[2 matches, ambiguous]` (new, currently lumped into "no match")
5. **No title match at all** — `[no matching title in other repo]` (more precise than "no match")

That means `find_matching_commit` needs to return richer info than just `Option<String>`. Maybe a result enum?

> DEVELOPER

sg, what about "err" status like:
err  zssouppsmspu  Refactor finalize into src/finalize.rs (0.7.0-dev2)  [wrong ID length, ID not found in other repo]

> AGENT

Right — `err` issues should also be more precise. For `wrong ID length`: show the actual vs expected length. For `ID not found`: show the ID that wasn't found. For `wrong prefix`: show the actual vs expected prefix.

So instead of `[wrong ID length, ID not found in other repo]` it would be something like:

```
err  zssouppsmspu  Refactor finalize...  [prefix: /mokrtwyz (expected /.claude/), id len: 8 (expected 12), id not found]
```

Hmm, that's verbose. Maybe:

```
err  zssouppsmspu  Refactor finalize...  [prefix: / (want /.claude/), len: 8 (want 12), not found]
```

What level of detail do you want?

> DEVELOPER

I'd lean towards versbose, atm

> AGENT

OK. So the plan is:

1. Change `OchidIssues` to carry the actual values (not just bools)
2. Change `find_matching_commit` to return a result enum with all the detail
3. Update both `validate-desc` and `fix-desc` to show the detailed messages
4. Add `--time-tolerance` flag or just widen the default?

For the time tolerance — should I add a flag, or is widening the default enough for now? The 29-minute gap suggests 60s is too strict for real-world use.

> DEVELOPER

Actually is the timestamp that of the initial commit, a squash description update or ...

> AGENT

Good question. The committer timestamp is whatever jj set it to — and `jj squash`, `jj describe`, `jj rebase` all update the committer timestamp. So the 29-minute gap could just mean one repo got squashed or described later than the other.

The author timestamp would be more stable (set at initial creation), but jj may update that too on some operations.

So the timestamp matching is fundamentally fragile for commits that have been rewritten. The title match alone might be the more reliable signal, with the timestamp as a tiebreaker when there are multiple title matches.

Should I flip the logic: match by title first, use timestamp only to disambiguate when multiple titles match?

> DEVELOPER

hmm, let's just use title but if there are multiple commits with the same title while fixing me would require an override, thoughts?

> AGENT

Makes sense. Title-only matching is simpler and more robust. The logic becomes:

- **One title match** → use it (validate shows it, fix-desc applies it)
- **Multiple title matches** → report them, don't auto-fix unless user provides an override (like `--force` or explicitly specifying the target changeID)
- **Zero title matches** → report no match

The timestamp tolerance code goes away entirely. The override for ambiguous matches could be a future feature — for now just report the count and skip.

This simplifies `find_matching_commit` significantly. Want me to proceed with all of this (richer error/match reporting + title-only matching) as a continuation of dev2 or as dev3?

> DEVELOPER

proceed as part of dev2 but record this discussion in chores

  "old_string": "/// Problems found with an ochid trailer.\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub struct OchidIssues {\n    pub wrong_prefix: bool,\n    pub wrong_length: bool,\n    pub not_found: bool,\n}\n\nimpl OchidIssues {\n    pub fn any(&self) -> bool {\n        self.wrong_prefix || self.wrong_length || self.not_found\n    }\n\n    pub fn summary(&self) -> String {\n        let mut parts = Vec::new();\n        if self.wrong_prefix {\n            parts.push(\"wrong prefix\");\n        }\n        if self.wrong_length {\n            parts.push(\"wrong ID length\");\n        }\n        if self.not_found {\n            parts.push(\"ID not found in other repo\");\n        }\n        parts.join(\", \")\n    }\n}\n\n/// Validate an ochid trailer value against expected prefix, length, and other repo.\npub fn validate_ochid(\n    value: &str,\n    other_prefix: &str,\n    id_len: usize,\n    other_workspace: &Workspace,\n    other_repo: &Arc<ReadonlyRepo>,\n) -> OchidIssues {\n    let wrong_prefix = !value.starts_with(other_prefix);\n\n    // Extract the bare ID\n    let bare_id = if let Some(pos) = value.rfind('/') {\n        &value[pos + 1..]\n    } else {\n        value\n    };\n\n    let wrong_length = bare_id.len() != id_len;\n\n    // Check if the ID resolves in the other repo\n    let not_found = common::resolve_revset(other_workspace, other_repo, bare_id)\n        .map(|ids| ids.is_empty())\n        .unwrap_or(true);\n\n    OchidIssues {\n        wrong_prefix,\n        wrong_length,\n        not_found,\n    }\n}",
  "new_string": "/// Problems found with an ochid trailer, with details for reporting.\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub struct OchidIssues {\n    /// None if prefix is correct, Some((actual_prefix, expected_prefix)) if wrong.\n    pub wrong_prefix: Option<(String, String)>,\n    /// None if length is correct, Some((actual_len, expected_len)) if wrong.\n    pub wrong_length: Option<(usize, usize)>,\n    /// true if the bare ID does not resolve in the other repo.\n    pub not_found: bool,\n    /// The bare ID extracted from the ochid value.\n    pub bare_id: String,\n}\n\nimpl OchidIssues {\n    pub fn any(&self) -> bool {\n        self.wrong_prefix.is_some() || self.wrong_length.is_some() || self.not_found\n    }\n\n    pub fn summary(&self) -> String {\n        let mut parts = Vec::new();\n        if let Some((ref actual, ref expected)) = self.wrong_prefix {\n            parts.push(format!(\"prefix: {actual} (want {expected})\"));\n        }\n        if let Some((actual, expected)) = self.wrong_length {\n            parts.push(format!(\"len: {actual} (want {expected})\"));\n        }\n        if self.not_found {\n            parts.push(format!(\"not found: {}\", self.bare_id));\n        }\n        parts.join(\", \")\n    }\n}\n\n/// Validate an ochid trailer value against expected prefix, length, and other repo.\npub fn validate_ochid(\n    value: &str,\n    other_prefix: &str,\n    id_len: usize,\n    other_workspace: &Workspace,\n    other_repo: &Arc<ReadonlyRepo>,\n) -> OchidIssues {\n    // Extract the actual prefix (everything before the bare ID)\n    let bare_id = extract_bare_id(value);\n    let actual_prefix = &value[..value.len() - bare_id.len()];\n\n    let wrong_prefix = if actual_prefix != other_prefix {\n        Some((actual_prefix.to_string(), other_prefix.to_string()))\n    } else {\n        None\n    };\n\n    let wrong_length = if bare_id.len() != id_len {\n        Some((bare_id.len(), id_len))\n    } else {\n        None\n    };\n\n    // Check if the ID resolves in the other repo\n    let not_found = common::resolve_revset(other_workspace, other_repo, bare_id)\n        .map(|ids| ids.is_empty())\n        .unwrap_or(true);\n\n    OchidIssues {\n        wrong_prefix,\n        wrong_length,\n        not_found,\n        bare_id: bare_id.to_string(),\n    }\n}"
}

  "old_string": "/// Maximum timestamp difference (in milliseconds) for title-based matching.\npub const TIMESTAMP_TOLERANCE_MS: i64 = 60_000;\n\n/// Find a unique matching commit in the other repo by title and timestamp.\n///\n/// Returns the other commit's change ID (full hex) if exactly one commit\n/// in the other repo has the same first description line and a committer\n/// timestamp within `TIMESTAMP_TOLERANCE_MS` of the source commit.\npub fn find_matching_commit(\n    commit: &Commit,\n    other_workspace: &Workspace,\n    other_repo: &Arc<ReadonlyRepo>,\n) -> Result<Option<String>, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let title = commit.description().lines().next().unwrap_or(\"\");\n    if title.is_empty() {\n        return Ok(None);\n    }\n    let src_millis = commit.committer().timestamp.timestamp.0;\n\n    // Search all commits in the other repo\n    let all_ids = common::resolve_revset(other_workspace, other_repo, \"all()\")?;\n    let root_id = other_repo.store().root_commit_id().clone();\n\n    let mut matches = Vec::new();\n    for cid in &all_ids {\n        if *cid == root_id {\n            continue;\n        }\n        let other_commit = other_repo.store().get_commit(cid)?;\n        let other_title = other_commit.description().lines().next().unwrap_or(\"\");\n        if other_title != title {\n            continue;\n        }\n        let other_millis = other_commit.committer().timestamp.timestamp.0;\n        if (src_millis - other_millis).abs() <= TIMESTAMP_TOLERANCE_MS {\n            let full_hex =\n                jj_lib::hex_util::encode_reverse_hex(other_commit.change_id().as_bytes());\n            matches.push(full_hex);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if matches.len() == 1 {\n        Ok(Some(matches.into_iter().next().unwrap()))\n    } else {\n        Ok(None)\n    }\n}",
  "new_string": "/// Result of searching the other repo for a matching commit by title.\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub enum TitleMatch {\n    /// No title to search for (empty description).\n    NoTitle,\n    /// Exactly one commit with the same title — unambiguous match.\n    One(String),\n    /// Multiple commits share the same title — ambiguous.\n    Ambiguous(usize),\n    /// No commit in the other repo has the same title.\n    None,\n}\n\n/// Find matching commits in the other repo by title (exact match).\n///\n/// Returns a `TitleMatch` describing the result.\npub fn find_matching_commit(\n    commit: &Commit,\n    other_workspace: &Workspace,\n    other_repo: &Arc<ReadonlyRepo>,\n) -> Result<TitleMatch, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let title = commit.description().lines().next().unwrap_or(\"\");\n    if title.is_empty() {\n        return Ok(TitleMatch::NoTitle);\n    }\n\n    // Search all commits in the other repo\n    let all_ids = common::resolve_revset(other_workspace, other_repo, \"all()\")?;\n    let root_id = other_repo.store().root_commit_id().clone();\n\n    let mut matches = Vec::new();\n    for cid in &all_ids {\n        if *cid == root_id {\n            continue;\n        }\n        let other_commit = other_repo.store().get_commit(cid)?;\n        let other_title = other_commit.description().lines().next().unwrap_or(\"\");\n        if other_title == title {\n            let full_hex =\n                jj_lib::hex_util::encode_reverse_hex(other_commit.change_id().as_bytes());\n            matches.push(full_hex);\n        }\n    }\n\n    match matches.len() {\n        0 => Ok(TitleMatch::None),\n        1 => Ok(TitleMatch::One(matches.into_iter().next().unwrap())),\n        n => Ok(TitleMatch::Ambiguous(n)),\n    }\n}"
}

<tool_use_error>String to replace not found in file.
String: /// Maximum timestamp difference (in milliseconds) for title-based matching.
pub const TIMESTAMP_TOLERANCE_MS: i64 = 60_000;

/// Find a unique matching commit in the other repo by title and timestamp.
///
/// Returns the other commit's change ID (full hex) if exactly one commit
/// in the other repo has the same first description line and a committer
/// timestamp within `TIMESTAMP_TOLERANCE_MS` of the source commit.
pub fn find_matching_commit(
    commit: &Commit,
    other_workspace: &Workspace,
    other_repo: &Arc<ReadonlyRepo>,
) -> Result<Option<String>, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    let title = commit.description().lines().next().unwrap_or("");
    if title.is_empty() {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    let src_millis = commit.committer().timestamp.timestamp.0;

    // Search all commits in the other repo
    let all_ids = common::resolve_revset(other_workspace, other_repo, "all()")?;
    let root_id = other_repo.store().root_commit_id().clone();

    let mut matches = Vec::new();
    for cid in &all_ids {
        if *cid == root_id {
            continue;
        }
        let other_commit = other_repo.store().get_commit(cid)?;
        let other_title = other_commit.description().lines().next().unwrap_or("");
        if other_title != title {
            continue;
        }
        let other_millis = other_commit.committer().timestamp.timestamp.0;
        if (src_millis - other_millis).abs() <= TIMESTAMP_TOLERANCE_MS {
            let full_hex =
                jj_lib::hex_util::encode_reverse_hex(other_commit.change_id().as_bytes());
            matches.push(full_hex);
        }
    }

    if matches.len() == 1 {
        Ok(Some(matches.into_iter().next().unwrap()))
    } else {
        Ok(None)
    }
}</tool_use_error>

  "old_string": "/// Find a unique matching commit in the other repo by title and timestamp.\n///\n/// Returns the other commit's change ID (full hex) if exactly one commit\n/// in the other repo has the same first description line and a committer\n/// timestamp within `TIMESTAMP_TOLERANCE_MS` of the source commit.\npub fn find_matching_commit(\n    commit: &Commit,\n    other_workspace: &Workspace,\n    other_repo: &Arc<ReadonlyRepo>,\n) -> Result<Option<String>, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let title = commit.description().lines().next().unwrap_or(\"\");\n    if title.is_empty() {\n        return Ok(None);\n    }\n    let src_millis = commit.committer().timestamp.timestamp.0;\n\n    // Search all commits in the other repo\n    let all_ids = common::resolve_revset(other_workspace, other_repo, \"all()\")?;\n    let root_id = other_repo.store().root_commit_id().clone();\n\n    let mut matches = Vec::new();\n    for cid in &all_ids {\n        if *cid == root_id {\n            continue;\n        }\n        let other_commit = other_repo.store().get_commit(cid)?;\n        let other_title = other_commit.description().lines().next().unwrap_or(\"\");\n        if other_title != title {\n            continue;\n        }\n        let other_millis = other_commit.committer().timestamp.timestamp.0;\n        if (src_millis - other_millis).abs() <= TIMESTAMP_TOLERANCE_MS {\n            let full_hex =\n                jj_lib::hex_util::encode_reverse_hex(other_commit.change_id().as_bytes());\n            matches.push(full_hex);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if matches.len() == 1 {\n        Ok(Some(matches.into_iter().next().unwrap()))\n    } else {\n        Ok(None)\n    }\n}",
  "new_string": "/// Result of searching the other repo for a matching commit by title.\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub enum TitleMatch {\n    /// No title to search for (empty description).\n    NoTitle,\n    /// Exactly one commit with the same title — unambiguous match.\n    One(String),\n    /// Multiple commits share the same title — ambiguous.\n    Ambiguous(usize),\n    /// No commit in the other repo has the same title.\n    None,\n}\n\n/// Find matching commits in the other repo by title (exact match).\npub fn find_matching_commit(\n    commit: &jj_lib::commit::Commit,\n    other_workspace: &Workspace,\n    other_repo: &Arc<ReadonlyRepo>,\n) -> Result<TitleMatch, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let title = commit.description().lines().next().unwrap_or(\"\");\n    if title.is_empty() {\n        return Ok(TitleMatch::NoTitle);\n    }\n\n    let all_ids = common::resolve_revset(other_workspace, other_repo, \"all()\")?;\n    let root_id = other_repo.store().root_commit_id().clone();\n\n    let mut matches = Vec::new();\n    for cid in &all_ids {\n        if *cid == root_id {\n            continue;\n        }\n        let other_commit = other_repo.store().get_commit(cid)?;\n        let other_title = other_commit.description().lines().next().unwrap_or(\"\");\n        if other_title == title {\n            let full_hex =\n                jj_lib::hex_util::encode_reverse_hex(other_commit.change_id().as_bytes());\n            matches.push(full_hex);\n        }\n    }\n\n    match matches.len() {\n        0 => Ok(TitleMatch::None),\n        1 => Ok(TitleMatch::One(matches.into_iter().next().unwrap())),\n        n => Ok(TitleMatch::Ambiguous(n)),\n    }\n}"
}

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/vc-x1/notes/chores-02.md",
  "old_string": "### 0.25.0-dev2 — Add fix-desc subcommand\n\nNew `fix-desc` subcommand using shared `desc_helpers`. Same positional arg\ninterface as `validate-desc` (`OTHER_REPO` required positional). Adds\n`--no-dry-run`, `--add-missing`, `--max-fixes`, `--fallback`, `--title`.\nExtracted `jj_describe` helper to reduce duplication. Marked `fix-ochid`\nas deprecated in help text.",
  "new_string": "### 0.25.0-dev2 — Add fix-desc subcommand\n\nNew `fix-desc` subcommand using shared `desc_helpers`. Same positional arg\ninterface as `validate-desc` (`OTHER_REPO` required positional). Adds\n`--no-dry-run`, `--add-missing`, `--max-fixes`, `--fallback`, `--title`.\nExtracted `jj_describe` helper to reduce duplication. Marked `fix-ochid`\nas deprecated in help text.\n\n#### Matching and error reporting improvements\n\nSwitched from timestamp+title matching to title-only matching. The original\n60-second timestamp tolerance was too strict — committer timestamps shift on\n`jj squash`, `jj describe`, and `jj rebase`, so commits made together can\ndiverge by minutes. Title-only matching is more robust.\n\nWhen multiple commits share the same title, the match is ambiguous and\nreported as `[N title matches, ambiguous]` — no auto-fix without an\nexplicit override (future feature).\n\n`OchidIssues` now carries actual values (not just bools) for verbose error\nreporting:\n- `prefix: / (want /.claude/)` instead of `wrong prefix`\n- `len: 8 (want 12)` instead of `wrong ID length`\n- `not found: ouoyxokq` instead of `ID not found in other repo`\n\n`TitleMatch` enum replaces `Option<String>` return from `find_matching_commit`,\ndistinguishing `NoTitle`, `One(id)`, `Ambiguous(n)`, and `None`."
}
```

[match: /xxx] my initial reaction is to terse I happen to know the issue is there is not ochid so it makes sense, but I can imagine there what if there was an ochid and prefix was correct bu the chid was wrong, what "reason" in that case?

> AGENT

Good point. Let me think through the cases for `[match: /ssokyszzwsxw]`:

- Currently it means: "no ochid trailer, but found a matching title → here's the likely counterpart"
- If there *was* an ochid but with the wrong changeID (right prefix, right length, but points to the wrong commit), that would show as `err` with `not found: <wrong-id>`

But the `miss` + `[match: ...]` doesn't tell you *why* it's showing that match. It should say something like:

```
miss slmkmroqtqtp  Unify ... (0.19.0)  [no ochid, title match: /ssokyszzwsxw]
```

That way it's clear: "there's no ochid trailer, but we found a title match you could use." Versus the err case which would be:

```
err  slmkmroqtqtp  Unify ... (0.19.0)  [not found: badid12345, title match: /ssokyszzwsxw]
```

Should I add title match suggestions to `err` cases too, or just make the `miss` messages more explicit for now?

> DEVELOPER

maybe "expected chid: xxxx" or "expectied: ohcid: /path/xxxx" showing the expected value?

> AGENT

That's cleaner — show what the ochid *should be*, not just that a match exists:

```
miss slmkmroqtqtp  Unify ... (0.19.0)  [no ochid, expected: ochid: /ssokyszzwsxw]
```

And for an err case where the ochid exists but points to the wrong ID:

```
err  slmkmroqtqtp  Unify ... (0.19.0)  [not found: badid12345, expected: ochid: /ssokyszzwsxw]
```

The `expected: ochid: /path/id` format mirrors what would actually go in the commit description. Should I add the title-match lookup to the `err` path too, or just `miss` for now?
